import {
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasPage,
  CanvasElement,
  CanvasTextRun,
  CanvasStyle,
  ScalePolicy,
  resolveScale,
  CanvasResource,
} from "../types.js";
import { FigmaNode, FigmaFileResponse, FigmaColor, FigmaPaint } from "../../parser/types.js";
import { isA4Ratio } from "../../pipeline/index.js";
import { SvgPath } from "../geometry/svgPathParser.js";
import { decodeBase64, decodeDataUrl } from "../utils/binaryUtils.js";

export interface FigmaCanvasParserOptions {
  imageMap?: Record<string, string>;
  selectedFrameIds?: string[];
  scalePolicy?: ScalePolicy;
  titleOverride?: string;
}

export class FigmaCanvasParser {
  private imageMap: Record<string, string>;
  private scalePolicy: ScalePolicy;
  private selectedFrameIds?: Set<string>;
  private titleOverride?: string;
  private resources: Record<string, CanvasResource> = {};
  private imageCounter = 0;
  private imageRefMap = new Map<string, string>(); // figmaRef -> resourceId

  constructor(options?: FigmaCanvasParserOptions) {
    this.imageMap = options?.imageMap || {};
    this.scalePolicy = options?.scalePolicy || "fit-a4";
    this.selectedFrameIds = options?.selectedFrameIds
      ? new Set(options.selectedFrameIds)
      : undefined;
    this.titleOverride = options?.titleOverride;
  }

  public parse(input: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse | any): CanvasDocument {
    this.resources = {};
    this.imageCounter = 0;
    this.imageRefMap.clear();

    if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) {
      throw new Error("Invalid Figma JSON input: expected a JSON object representing a Figma node, file response, or snapshot.");
    }

    if ("imageMap" in input && input.imageMap && typeof input.imageMap === "object") {
      this.imageMap = { ...input.imageMap, ...this.imageMap };
    }

    let rootNode: FigmaNode;
    if ("figmaNode" in input && input.figmaNode && typeof input.figmaNode === "object") {
      rootNode = input.figmaNode;
    } else if ("document" in input && input.document && typeof input.document === "object") {
      rootNode = input.document;
    } else if ("nodes" in input && input.nodes && typeof input.nodes === "object") {
      const firstEntry = Object.values(input.nodes)[0] as any;
      rootNode = firstEntry?.document || firstEntry;
    } else {
      rootNode = input as FigmaNode;
    }

    if (!rootNode || typeof rootNode !== "object" || (!rootNode.type && !Array.isArray(rootNode.children))) {
      throw new Error("Invalid Figma JSON structure: missing valid root Figma node ('type' or 'children').");
    }

    const documentName =
      this.titleOverride ||
      (input.metadata?.fileName ? String(input.metadata.fileName) : "") ||
      ("name" in input && input.name ? String(input.name) : "") ||
      (rootNode.name ? String(rootNode.name) : "Figma Document");

    const notices: string[] = [];
    const warnings: string[] = [];

    // Step 1: Discover top-level frames representing document pages
    const pageFrames = this.discoverPageFrames(rootNode);

    if (pageFrames.length === 0) {
      warnings.push("No candidate page frames found. Using root as single page.");
    }

    const pages: CanvasPage[] = [];

    // If no page frames found, treat root itself as a page
    const framesToProcess = pageFrames.length > 0 ? pageFrames : [rootNode];

    for (let i = 0; i < framesToProcess.length; i++) {
      const frame = framesToProcess[i];
      const pageIndex = i + 1;
      const page = this.parsePageFrame(frame, pageIndex, notices, warnings);
      pages.push(page);
    }

    const primaryWidthPt = pages.length > 0 ? pages[0].bounds.width : 595.28;
    const primaryHeightPt = pages.length > 0 ? pages[0].bounds.height : 841.89;
    const isLandscape = primaryWidthPt > primaryHeightPt;
    const isA4 = Math.abs(primaryWidthPt * primaryHeightPt - 595.28 * 841.89) < 200;
    const pageSizeName = isA4 ? "A4" : "Custom";

    return {
      title: documentName,
      pageSize: {
        name: pageSizeName,
        widthPt: Number(primaryWidthPt.toFixed(2)),
        heightPt: Number(primaryHeightPt.toFixed(2)),
        orientation: isLandscape ? "landscape" : "portrait",
        unit: "pt",
      },
      marginsPt: {
        top: 0,
        right: 0,
        bottom: 0,
        left: 0,
      },
      pages,
      resources: this.resources,
      diagnostics: {
        notices,
        warnings,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Discovers top-level frames that qualify as document pages (e.g. A4 frames).
   */
  private discoverPageFrames(root: FigmaNode): FigmaNode[] {
    const candidateFrames: FigmaNode[] = [];

    const visitCanvasOrDoc = (node: FigmaNode) => {
      if (!node.children) return;

      if (node.type === "CANVAS") {
        const visChildren = node.children.filter((c) => c.visible !== false);
        const frameChildren = visChildren.filter(
          (c) => c.type === "FRAME" || c.type === "COMPONENT" || c.type === "GROUP"
        );
        const leafChildren = visChildren.filter(
          (c) =>
            c.type === "TEXT" ||
            c.type === "LINE" ||
            c.type === "RECTANGLE" ||
            c.type === "VECTOR" ||
            c.type === "ELLIPSE"
        );
        const allFramesSelected =
          !this.selectedFrameIds ||
          frameChildren.every((f) => this.selectedFrameIds!.has(f.id));
        const allFramesAreShortSections =
          frameChildren.length >= 1 &&
          frameChildren.every(
            (f) =>
              (f.absoluteBoundingBox?.height || 0) > 0 &&
              (f.absoluteBoundingBox?.height || 0) < 500
          );
        const hasMixedLeafAndFrames = leafChildren.length > 0 && frameChildren.length > 0;

        if (
          allFramesSelected &&
          allFramesAreShortSections &&
          (hasMixedLeafAndFrames || frameChildren.length >= 2)
        ) {
          let minX = Infinity;
          let minY = Infinity;
          let maxX = -Infinity;
          let maxY = -Infinity;
          for (const c of visChildren) {
            const b = c.absoluteBoundingBox;
            if (b) {
              if (b.x < minX) minX = b.x;
              if (b.y < minY) minY = b.y;
              if (b.x + b.width > maxX) maxX = b.x + b.width;
              if (b.y + b.height > maxY) maxY = b.y + b.height;
            }
          }
          if (!isFinite(minX)) {
            minX = 0;
            minY = 0;
            maxX = 794;
            maxY = 1123;
          }
          const spanW = Math.max(794, maxX - minX);
          const spanH = Math.max(maxY - minY + 48, Math.round(spanW * (841.89 / 595.28)));
          candidateFrames.push({
            ...node,
            type: "FRAME",
            absoluteBoundingBox: { x: minX, y: minY, width: spanW, height: spanH },
            children: visChildren,
          });
          return;
        }
      }

      for (const child of node.children) {
        if (child.visible === false) continue;

        if (child.type === "CANVAS") {
          visitCanvasOrDoc(child);
          continue;
        }

        // If frame selection filter is active
        if (this.selectedFrameIds && !this.selectedFrameIds.has(child.id)) {
          continue;
        }

        // Check if child is a frame or component
        if (
          child.type === "FRAME" ||
          child.type === "COMPONENT" ||
          child.type === "GROUP"
        ) {
          candidateFrames.push(child);
        }
      }
    };

    if (root.type === "DOCUMENT" || root.type === "CANVAS") {
      visitCanvasOrDoc(root);
    } else {
      candidateFrames.push(root);
    }

    // Sort candidate frames in reading order (top-to-bottom, left-to-right)
    candidateFrames.sort((a, b) => {
      const boxA = a.absoluteBoundingBox || { x: 0, y: 0 };
      const boxB = b.absoluteBoundingBox || { x: 0, y: 0 };
      const rowA = Math.round(boxA.y / 100);
      const rowB = Math.round(boxB.y / 100);
      if (rowA !== rowB) return rowA - rowB;
      return boxA.x - boxB.x;
    });

    return candidateFrames;
  }

  /**
   * Synthesizes deterministic absoluteBoundingBox coordinates for child nodes inside
   * Auto-Layout frames (layoutMode: VERTICAL | HORIZONTAL) when absoluteBoundingBox is omitted.
   */
  private synthesizeMissingChildBounds(
    parent: FigmaNode,
    parentBox: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }
  ): void {
    if (!parent.children || parent.children.length === 0) return;

    const pl = (parent as any).paddingLeft || 0;
    const pr = (parent as any).paddingRight || 0;
    const pt = (parent as any).paddingTop || 0;
    const pb = (parent as any).paddingBottom || 0;
    const gap = (parent as any).itemSpacing || 12;
    const layoutMode = (parent as any).layoutMode || "VERTICAL";

    const innerX = parentBox.x + pl;
    const innerY = parentBox.y + pt;
    const innerW = Math.max(20, parentBox.width - pl - pr);
    const innerH = Math.max(16, parentBox.height - pt - pb);

    const visChildren = parent.children.filter((c) => c.visible !== false);
    if (visChildren.length === 0) return;

    if (layoutMode === "HORIZONTAL") {
      let cursorX = innerX;
      const missingCount = visChildren.filter((c) => !c.absoluteBoundingBox).length;
      const usedW = visChildren.reduce(
        (acc, c) => acc + (c.absoluteBoundingBox ? c.absoluteBoundingBox.width + gap : 0),
        0
      );
      const remW = Math.max(40, innerW - usedW);
      const defaultColW =
        missingCount > 0
          ? Math.max(40, (remW - gap * Math.max(0, missingCount - 1)) / missingCount)
          : innerW;

      for (const child of visChildren) {
        if (!child.absoluteBoundingBox) {
          let cw = defaultColW;
          let ch = innerH;
          if (child.type === "TEXT") {
            const fs = child.style?.fontSize || 12;
            const lh = child.style?.lineHeightPx || Math.round(fs * 1.35);
            ch = Math.min(innerH, Math.max(lh + 4, innerH));
          }
          (child as any).absoluteBoundingBox = {
            x: cursorX,
            y: innerY,
            width: cw,
            height: ch,
          };
        }
        const cb = child.absoluteBoundingBox!;
        cursorX = Math.max(cursorX, cb.x + cb.width + gap);
        this.synthesizeMissingChildBounds(child, cb);
      }
    } else {
      let cursorY = innerY;
      for (const child of visChildren) {
        if (!child.absoluteBoundingBox) {
          let cw = innerW;
          let ch = 32;

          if (child.type === "TEXT") {
            const fs = child.style?.fontSize || 12;
            const lh = child.style?.lineHeightPx || Math.round(fs * 1.4);
            const chars = child.characters || "";
            const charsPerLine = Math.max(8, Math.floor(innerW / (fs * 0.72)));
            const explicitLines = chars.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
            const totalLines = explicitLines.reduce(
              (sum, l) => sum + Math.max(1, Math.ceil(l.length / charsPerLine)),
              0
            );
            cw = innerW;
            ch = Math.max(lh + 4, totalLines * lh + 4);
          } else if (
            child.type === "FRAME" ||
            child.type === "COMPONENT" ||
            child.type === "GROUP"
          ) {
            const cpl = (child as any).paddingLeft || 0;
            const cpr = (child as any).paddingRight || 0;
            const cpt = (child as any).paddingTop || 0;
            const cpb = (child as any).paddingBottom || 0;
            if (
              (child as any).primaryAxisSizingMode === "AUTO" &&
              child.children &&
              child.children.length === 1 &&
              child.children[0].type === "TEXT"
            ) {
              const tNode = child.children[0];
              const fs = tNode.style?.fontSize || 12;
              const lh = tNode.style?.lineHeightPx || Math.round(fs * 1.35);
              const tLen = (tNode.characters || "").length;
              cw = Math.min(innerW, Math.max(60, Math.round(tLen * fs * 0.8 + cpl + cpr + 12)));
              ch = lh + cpt + cpb + 4;
            } else {
              cw = innerW;
              ch = Math.max(48, Math.min(200, innerH - Math.max(0, cursorY - innerY)));
            }
          }

          (child as any).absoluteBoundingBox = {
            x: innerX,
            y: cursorY,
            width: cw,
            height: ch,
          };
        }
        const cb = child.absoluteBoundingBox!;
        cursorY = Math.max(cursorY, cb.y + cb.height + gap);
        this.synthesizeMissingChildBounds(child, cb);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Parses a single top-level frame into a CanvasPage.
   */
  private parsePageFrame(
    frame: FigmaNode,
    pageNumber: number,
    notices: string[],
    warnings: string[]
  ): CanvasPage {
    const frameBox = frame.absoluteBoundingBox || {
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      width: 794,
      height: 1123,
    };

    // Synthesize bounds for Auto-Layout child nodes that omit explicit absoluteBoundingBox
    this.synthesizeMissingChildBounds(frame, frameBox);

    const resolved = resolveScale(
      frameBox.width,
      frameBox.height,
      this.scalePolicy
    );
    const { scaleX, scaleY, targetWidthPt, targetHeightPt } = resolved;

    // Check background color
    let backgroundColor: string | undefined;
    const rawBg = (frame as any).backgroundColor;
    if (rawBg) {
      backgroundColor = this.figmaColorToHex(rawBg);
    } else if (frame.fills && frame.fills.length > 0) {
      const solid = frame.fills.find(
        (f) => f.type === "SOLID" && f.visible !== false
      );
      if (solid && solid.color) {
        backgroundColor = this.figmaColorToHex(solid.color);
      }
    }

    const elements: CanvasElement[] = [];
    let currentZIndex = 1;

    // Helper to recursively process children
    const processChildren = (node: FigmaNode) => {
      if (!node.children) return;

      for (const child of node.children) {
        if (child.visible === false) continue;

        const childBox = child.absoluteBoundingBox;
        if (!childBox) {
          // If no absolute bounding box, still process children if any
          processChildren(child);
          continue;
        }

        // Relative coordinate to page frame
        const relX = childBox.x - frameBox.x;
        const relY = childBox.y - frameBox.y;

        const xPt = relX * scaleX;
        const yPt = relY * scaleY;
        const wPt = childBox.width * scaleX;
        const hPt = childBox.height * scaleY;

        // Diagnostic: clipsContent check
        if (child.clipsContent) {
          notices.push(
            `Page ${pageNumber}, node ${child.name || child.id} has clipsContent=true.`
          );
        }

        // Check if node is an image fill
        const imageFill = child.fills?.find(
          (f) => f.type === "IMAGE" && f.visible !== false && f.imageRef
        );

        if (imageFill && imageFill.imageRef) {
          const resId = this.resolveImageResource(imageFill.imageRef);
          const scaleModeRaw = (imageFill as any).scaleMode as
            | "FILL"
            | "FIT"
            | "CROP"
            | "TILE"
            | undefined;
          const imageScaleMode = scaleModeRaw || "FILL";
          let imageCrop:
            | { top: number; bottom: number; left: number; right: number }
            | undefined;

          const transform = (imageFill as any).imageTransform as
            | [[number, number, number], [number, number, number]]
            | undefined;
          if (
            imageScaleMode === "CROP" &&
            Array.isArray(transform) &&
            transform.length === 2 &&
            Array.isArray(transform[0]) &&
            Array.isArray(transform[1])
          ) {
            const sx = transform[0][0] ?? 1;
            const tx = transform[0][2] ?? 0;
            const sy = transform[1][1] ?? 1;
            const ty = transform[1][2] ?? 0;
            let left = 0,
              top = 0,
              right = 0,
              bottom = 0;
            if (tx >= 0 && ty >= 0 && sx > 0 && sx <= 1 && sy > 0 && sy <= 1) {
              left = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, tx));
              top = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, ty));
              right = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (tx + sx)));
              bottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (ty + sy)));
            } else if (sx > 0 && sy > 0) {
              left = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, -tx / sx));
              top = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, -ty / sy));
              right = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (1 - tx) / sx));
              bottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (1 - ty) / sy));
            }
            if (left > 0 || top > 0 || right > 0 || bottom > 0) {
              imageCrop = { top, bottom, left, right };
            }
          }

          elements.push({
            id: child.id,
            name: child.name,
            type: "image",
            bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
            zIndex: currentZIndex++,
            rotation: (child as any).rotation,
            opacity: child.opacity,
            style: this.extractStyle(child, scaleX),
            imageRef: resId,
            imageScaleMode,
            imageCrop,
            sourceNodeId: child.id,
            sourceNodeType: child.type,
          });
        } else if (child.type === "TEXT") {
          const textRuns = this.extractTextRuns(child, scaleX);
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const align = this.extractTextAlign(child);

          let verticalAlign: "top" | "center" | "bottom" | undefined;
          const vAlignRaw = child.style?.textAlignVertical || (child as any).textAlignVertical;
          if (vAlignRaw === "CENTER") {
            verticalAlign = "center";
          } else if (vAlignRaw === "BOTTOM") {
            verticalAlign = "bottom";
          } else if (vAlignRaw === "TOP") {
            verticalAlign = "top";
          }

          let isCenteredInParent = false;
          let textYPt = yPt;
          let textHPt = hPt;
          const parentBox = node.absoluteBoundingBox;

          // Check if text is vertically centered in parent container (badge, button, table cell, row)
          if (
            node.id !== frame.id &&
            parentBox &&
            childBox &&
            parentBox.height < 600 &&
            parentBox.height > childBox.height + 2
          ) {
            const topGap = childBox.y - parentBox.y;
            const bottomGap = (parentBox.y + parentBox.height) - (childBox.y + childBox.height);
            const isSymmetric = Math.abs(topGap - bottomGap) <= 2.5;

            // Only consider centered if parent is NOT a vertical stack of multiple children
            const isVerticalStack =
              (node as any).layoutMode === "VERTICAL" &&
              node.children &&
              node.children.length > 1;

            if (isSymmetric && !isVerticalStack) {
              isCenteredInParent = true;
              verticalAlign = "center";
              const parentRelY = parentBox.y - frameBox.y;
              textYPt = parentRelY * scaleY;
              textHPt = parentBox.height * scaleY;
            }
          }

          // For Figma TEXT nodes, 'fills' represents character font colors (stored in textRuns),
          // NOT a container background fill. Remove fillColor so the text box has a transparent background.
          delete style.fillColor;
          delete style.fillOpacity;

          const padTop = ((child as any).paddingTop || 0) * scaleY;
          const padRight = ((child as any).paddingRight || 0) * scaleX;
          const padBottom = ((child as any).paddingBottom || 0) * scaleY;
          const padLeft = ((child as any).paddingLeft || 0) * scaleX;
          const innerPaddingPt =
            padTop > 0 || padRight > 0 || padBottom > 0 || padLeft > 0
              ? { top: padTop, right: padRight, bottom: padBottom, left: padLeft }
              : undefined;

          const rawTar = (child.style as any)?.textAutoResize || (child as any).textAutoResize;
          const textAutoResize: "NONE" | "HEIGHT" | "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT" | "TRUNCATE" | undefined =
            rawTar === "HEIGHT" || rawTar === "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT" || rawTar === "NONE" || rawTar === "TRUNCATE"
              ? rawTar
              : undefined;

          elements.push({
            id: child.id,
            name: child.name,
            type: "text",
            bounds: { x: xPt, y: textYPt, width: wPt, height: textHPt },
            zIndex: currentZIndex++,
            rotation: (child as any).rotation,
            opacity: child.opacity,
            style,
            textRuns,
            paragraphAlign: align,
            verticalAlign,
            innerPaddingPt,
            textAutoResize,
            contentHeightPt: isCenteredInParent ? hPt : undefined,
            sourceNodeId: child.id,
            sourceNodeType: child.type,
          });
        } else if (child.type === "LINE") {
          let lineW = wPt;
          let lineH = hPt;
          const rawRot = (child as any).rotation;
          if (typeof rawRot === "number" && lineW > 0 && lineH === 0) {
            const absRot = Math.abs(rawRot);
            if (Math.abs(absRot - Math.PI / 2) < 0.1 || Math.abs(absRot - 90) < 5) {
              lineH = lineW;
              lineW = 0;
            }
          }
          elements.push({
            id: child.id,
            name: child.name,
            type: "line",
            bounds: {
              x: xPt,
              y: yPt,
              width: lineW,
              height: lineW === 0 && lineH > 0 ? lineH : Math.max(lineH, 1),
            },
            zIndex: currentZIndex++,
            rotation: rawRot,
            opacity: child.opacity,
            style: this.extractStyle(child, scaleX),
            sourceNodeId: child.id,
            sourceNodeType: child.type,
          });
        } else if (child.type === "ELLIPSE") {
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const hasFill = !!style.fillColor;
          const hasStroke = !!style.strokeColor && (style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;
          if (hasFill || hasStroke) {
            const arcData = (child as any).arcData as
              | { startingAngle?: number; endingAngle?: number; innerRadius?: number }
              | undefined;
            const innerRadius = arcData?.innerRadius ?? 0;
            const startAngle = arcData?.startingAngle ?? 0;
            const endAngle = arcData?.endingAngle ?? Math.PI * 2;
            const sweepDelta = endAngle - startAngle;
            const isPartialArc =
              arcData !== undefined &&
              Math.abs(Math.abs(sweepDelta) - Math.PI * 2) > 0.01;
            const isDonutOrSector = innerRadius > 0.001 || isPartialArc;

            if (isDonutOrSector && Math.abs(sweepDelta) > 1e-4) {
              const rawPath = this.buildEllipseArcSvgPath(
                startAngle,
                endAngle,
                innerRadius
              );
              const parsed = SvgPath.parse(rawPath);
              const unitBounds = {
                minX: 0,
                minY: 0,
                maxX: 1000,
                maxY: 1000,
                width: 1000,
                height: 1000,
              };
              const vmlPath = parsed.toVmlPath(1000, 1000, unitBounds);
              const wHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(wPt * 100));
              const hHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(hPt * 100));
              const vectorSubpaths = parsed.toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(
                wHwpu,
                hHwpu,
                6,
                unitBounds
              );
              const vectorPoints = vectorSubpaths.flatMap((s) => s.points);

              elements.push({
                id: child.id,
                name: child.name,
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: (child as any).rotation,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style,
                vectorPath: vmlPath,
                rawSvgPath: rawPath,
                vectorPoints,
                vectorSubpaths,
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            } else {
              elements.push({
                id: child.id,
                name: child.name,
                type: "oval",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: (child as any).rotation,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style,
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
          }
          processChildren(child);
        } else if (
          child.type === "VECTOR" ||
          child.type === "STAR" ||
          child.type === "POLYGON" ||
          child.type === "BOOLEAN_OPERATION"
        ) {
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const hasFill = !!style.fillColor;
          const hasStroke = !!style.strokeColor && (style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

          if (hasFill || hasStroke) {
            const parentName = node.name || "";
            const nodeName = child.name || "";
            let rawPath = "";

            if ((child as any).fillGeometry?.[0]?.path) {
              rawPath = (child as any).fillGeometry[0].path;
            } else if ((child as any).strokeGeometry?.[0]?.path) {
              rawPath = (child as any).strokeGeometry[0].path;
            } else if ((child as any).vectorPaths?.[0]?.data) {
              rawPath = (child as any).vectorPaths[0].data;
            } else if (child.type === "STAR") {
              rawPath = "M 500 0 L 618 353 L 990 353 L 689 572 L 804 925 L 500 706 L 196 925 L 311 572 L 10 353 L 382 353 Z";
            } else if (child.type === "POLYGON") {
              rawPath = "M 500 0 L 1000 866 L 0 866 Z";
            } else {
              // Standard UI icon path normalization (SVG format)
              const nameLower = (parentName + " " + nodeName).toLowerCase();
              if (nameLower.includes("alert") || nameLower.includes("warn") || nameLower.includes("triangle")) {
                rawPath = "M 500 60 L 950 920 L 50 920 Z M 500 380 L 500 640 M 500 780 L 500 840";
              } else if (nameLower.includes("check") || nameLower.includes("tick")) {
                rawPath = "M 150 520 L 400 780 L 880 180";
              } else if (nameLower.includes("info")) {
                rawPath = "M 500 50 C 748 50 950 252 950 500 C 950 748 748 950 500 950 C 252 950 50 748 50 500 C 50 252 252 50 500 50 Z M 500 280 L 500 340 M 500 460 L 500 740";
              } else if (nameLower.includes("file") || nameLower.includes("doc")) {
                rawPath = "M 200 80 L 620 80 L 820 280 L 820 920 L 200 920 Z M 620 80 L 620 280 L 820 280 M 340 480 L 680 480 M 340 640 L 680 640";
              }
            }

            let vmlPath = "";
            let vectorPoints: { x: number; y: number }[] | undefined;
            let vectorSubpaths: { points: { x: number; y: number }[]; closed: boolean }[] | undefined;

            if (rawPath) {
              const parsed = SvgPath.parse(rawPath);
              vmlPath = parsed.toVmlPath(1000, 1000);
              const wHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(wPt * 100));
              const hHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(hPt * 100));
              vectorSubpaths = parsed.toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(wHwpu, hHwpu);
              vectorPoints = vectorSubpaths.flatMap((s) => s.points);
            }

            elements.push({
              id: child.id,
              name: child.name,
              type: "vector",
              bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
              zIndex: currentZIndex++,
              rotation: (child as any).rotation,
              opacity: child.opacity,
              style,
              vectorPath: vmlPath,
              rawSvgPath: rawPath,
              vectorPoints,
              vectorSubpaths,
              sourceNodeId: child.id,
              sourceNodeType: child.type,
            });
          }
          processChildren(child);
        } else if (
          child.type === "RECTANGLE" ||
          child.type === "FRAME" ||
          child.type === "COMPONENT" ||
          child.type === "INSTANCE"
        ) {
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const hasFill = !!style.fillColor;
          const hasStroke = !!style.strokeColor && (style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

          const bs = style.borderSides;
          const isPartialBorder =
            hasStroke &&
            bs !== undefined &&
            ((bs.top ?? 0) === 0 ||
              (bs.right ?? 0) === 0 ||
              (bs.bottom ?? 0) === 0 ||
              (bs.left ?? 0) === 0);

          if (isPartialBorder) {
            // 1. If has background fill, emit a card with fill only (no 4-sided stroke)
            if (hasFill) {
              elements.push({
                id: child.id,
                name: child.name,
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: (child as any).rotation,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  fillColor: style.fillColor,
                  fillOpacity: style.fillOpacity,
                  cornerRadius: style.cornerRadius,
                  cornerRadii: style.cornerRadii,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }

            // 2. Emit separate line elements for the active border sides
            const strokeColor = style.strokeColor;
            const strokeDashed = style.strokeDashed;

            if ((bs.bottom ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_b`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_bottom`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt + hPt, width: wPt, height: 1 },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.bottom,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
            if ((bs.top ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_t`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_top`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: 1 },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.top,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
            if ((bs.left ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_l`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_left`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: 1, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.left,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
            if ((bs.right ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_r`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_right`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt + wPt, y: yPt, width: 1, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.right,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
          } else if (hasFill || hasStroke) {
            elements.push({
              id: child.id,
              name: child.name,
              type: "card",
              bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
              zIndex: currentZIndex++,
              rotation: (child as any).rotation,
              opacity: child.opacity,
              style,
              sourceNodeId: child.id,
              sourceNodeType: child.type,
            });
          }

          // Process nested children inside this container
          processChildren(child);
        } else if (child.type === "GROUP") {
          processChildren(child);
        } else {
          // Fallback for VECTOR, STAR, ELLIPSE, etc.
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          if (style.fillColor || style.strokeColor) {
            elements.push({
              id: child.id,
              name: child.name,
              type: "card",
              bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
              zIndex: currentZIndex++,
              rotation: (child as any).rotation,
              opacity: child.opacity,
              style,
              sourceNodeId: child.id,
              sourceNodeType: child.type,
            });
          }
          processChildren(child);
        }
      }
    };

    processChildren(frame);

    // Sort elements by zIndex
    elements.sort((a, b) => a.zIndex - b.zIndex);

    return {
      pageNumber,
      name: frame.name || `Page ${pageNumber}`,
      bounds: {
        x: 0,
        y: 0,
        width: targetWidthPt,
        height: targetHeightPt,
      },
      backgroundColor,
      elements,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Resolves a Figma imageRef to a local CanvasResource.
   */
  private resolveImageResource(imageRef: string): string {
    if (this.imageRefMap.has(imageRef)) {
      return this.imageRefMap.get(imageRef)!;
    }

    this.imageCounter++;
    const resId = `image${this.imageCounter}.png`;
    const dataUrl = this.imageMap[imageRef];

    let data: Uint8Array;
    let mimeType = "image/png";

    if (dataUrl && dataUrl.startsWith("data:")) {
      const decoded = decodeDataUrl(dataUrl);
      if (decoded) {
        mimeType = decoded.mime || "image/png";
        data = decoded.bytes;
      } else {
        data = new Uint8Array(0);
      }
    } else {
      // 1x1 transparent placeholder
      data = decodeBase64(
        "iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAQAAAC1HAwCAAAAC0lEQVR42mNkYAAAAAYAAjCB0C8AAAAASUVORK5CYII="
      );
    }

    this.resources[resId] = {
      id: resId,
      name: resId,
      mimeType,
      data,
    };

    this.imageRefMap.set(imageRef, resId);
    return resId;
  }

  /**
   * Extracts visual style (fill, stroke, corner radius) from a Figma node.
   */
  private extractStyle(node: FigmaNode, scale: number): CanvasStyle {
    const style: CanvasStyle = {};

    // 1. Fills
    if (node.fills && node.fills.length > 0) {
      const solid = node.fills.find(
        (f) => f.type === "SOLID" && f.visible !== false
      );
      if (solid && solid.color) {
        style.fillColor = this.figmaColorToHex(solid.color);
        style.fillOpacity = solid.opacity ?? solid.color.a ?? 1.0;
      }
    }

    // 2. Strokes
    const rawWeight = typeof node.strokeWeight === "number" ? node.strokeWeight : 1;
    const isw = (node as any).individualStrokeWeights as
      | { top?: number; right?: number; bottom?: number; left?: number }
      | undefined;

    let hasNonZeroWeight = rawWeight > 0;
    if (isw) {
      style.borderSides = {
        top: typeof isw.top === "number" ? isw.top * scale : undefined,
        right: typeof isw.right === "number" ? isw.right * scale : undefined,
        bottom: typeof isw.bottom === "number" ? isw.bottom * scale : undefined,
        left: typeof isw.left === "number" ? isw.left * scale : undefined,
      };
      hasNonZeroWeight =
        (style.borderSides.top ?? 0) > 0 ||
        (style.borderSides.right ?? 0) > 0 ||
        (style.borderSides.bottom ?? 0) > 0 ||
        (style.borderSides.left ?? 0) > 0;
    }

    if (hasNonZeroWeight && node.strokes && node.strokes.length > 0) {
      const stroke = node.strokes.find(
        (s) => s.type === "SOLID" && s.visible !== false
      );
      if (stroke && stroke.color) {
        style.strokeColor = this.figmaColorToHex(stroke.color);
        style.strokeWidth = rawWeight * scale;
        const dashes = (node as any).strokeDashes;
        if (dashes && dashes.length > 0) {
          style.strokeDashed = true;
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Corner Radius
    if (
      Array.isArray(node.rectangleCornerRadii) &&
      node.rectangleCornerRadii.length === 4
    ) {
      const radii = node.rectangleCornerRadii.map((r) => (r || 0) * scale) as [
        number,
        number,
        number,
        number
      ];
      style.cornerRadii = radii;
      const maxR = Math.max(...radii);
      if (maxR > 0) {
        const avgR = (radii[0] + radii[1] + radii[2] + radii[3]) / 4;
        style.cornerRadius = avgR > 0 ? avgR : maxR;
      }
    }
    if (node.cornerRadius) {
      style.cornerRadius = node.cornerRadius * scale;
    }

    return style;
  }

  /**
   * Builds an exact SVG path in [0..1000, 0..1000] coordinate space for an ELLIPSE node
   * that defines Figma arcData (donut sector or pie slice).
   */
  private buildEllipseArcSvgPath(
    startAngle: number,
    endAngle: number,
    innerRadius: number
  ): string {
    const cx = 500;
    const cy = 500;
    const outerR = 500;
    const innerR = Math.max(0, Math.min(499, outerR * innerRadius));
    const delta = endAngle - startAngle;
    const segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.abs(delta) / (Math.PI / 2)));
    const dTheta = delta / segments;
    const alpha = (4 / 3) * Math.tan(dTheta / 4);

    const parts: string[] = [];

    const x0 = cx + outerR * Math.cos(startAngle);
    const y0 = cy + outerR * Math.sin(startAngle);
    parts.push(`M ${x0.toFixed(3)} ${y0.toFixed(3)}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
      const t0 = startAngle + i * dTheta;
      const t1 = t0 + dTheta;
      const p0x = cx + outerR * Math.cos(t0);
      const p0y = cy + outerR * Math.sin(t0);
      const p1x = cx + outerR * Math.cos(t1);
      const p1y = cy + outerR * Math.sin(t1);
      const cp1x = p0x - alpha * outerR * Math.sin(t0);
      const cp1y = p0y + alpha * outerR * Math.cos(t0);
      const cp2x = p1x + alpha * outerR * Math.sin(t1);
      const cp2y = p1y - alpha * outerR * Math.cos(t1);
      parts.push(
        `C ${cp1x.toFixed(3)} ${cp1y.toFixed(3)} ${cp2x.toFixed(3)} ${cp2y.toFixed(3)} ${p1x.toFixed(3)} ${p1y.toFixed(3)}`
      );
    }

    if (innerR > 0.5) {
      const ixEnd = cx + innerR * Math.cos(endAngle);
      const iyEnd = cy + innerR * Math.sin(endAngle);
      parts.push(`L ${ixEnd.toFixed(3)} ${iyEnd.toFixed(3)}`);

      const revAlpha = -alpha;
      for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
        const t0 = endAngle - i * dTheta;
        const t1 = t0 - dTheta;
        const p0x = cx + innerR * Math.cos(t0);
        const p0y = cy + innerR * Math.sin(t0);
        const p1x = cx + innerR * Math.cos(t1);
        const p1y = cy + innerR * Math.sin(t1);
        const cp1x = p0x - revAlpha * innerR * Math.sin(t0);
        const cp1y = p0y + revAlpha * innerR * Math.cos(t0);
        const cp2x = p1x + revAlpha * innerR * Math.sin(t1);
        const cp2y = p1y - revAlpha * innerR * Math.cos(t1);
        parts.push(
          `C ${cp1x.toFixed(3)} ${cp1y.toFixed(3)} ${cp2x.toFixed(3)} ${cp2y.toFixed(3)} ${p1x.toFixed(3)} ${p1y.toFixed(3)}`
        );
      }
      parts.push("Z");
    } else {
      parts.push(`L ${cx} ${cy} Z`);
    }

    return parts.join(" ");
  }

  /**
   * Extracts text runs from a Figma TEXT node, taking into account
   * character style overrides if present.
   */
  private extractTextRuns(node: FigmaNode, scale: number): CanvasTextRun[] {
    const characters = node.characters || "";
    if (!characters) return [];

    const defaultFontFamily = node.style?.fontFamily || "Inter";
    const defaultFontSize = (node.style?.fontSize || 12) * scale;
    const rawWeight = node.style?.fontWeight;
    const defaultFontWeight =
      typeof rawWeight === "number"
        ? rawWeight
        : rawWeight
        ? parseInt(String(rawWeight), 10) || 400
        : 400;
    const defaultItalic =
      node.style?.italic ||
      node.style?.fontPostScriptName?.toLowerCase().includes("italic") ||
      false;
    const defaultUnderline = node.style?.textDecoration === "UNDERLINE";
    const defaultStrike = node.style?.textDecoration === "STRIKETHROUGH";

    const lineHeightUnit = (node.style as any)?.lineHeightUnit as string | undefined;
    let defaultLineHeightPt: number | undefined;
    if (lineHeightUnit !== "INTRINSIC_%") {
      if (typeof node.style?.lineHeightPx === "number" && node.style.lineHeightPx > 0) {
        defaultLineHeightPt = node.style.lineHeightPx * scale;
      } else if (
        typeof (node.style as any)?.lineHeightPercentFontSize === "number" &&
        (node.style as any).lineHeightPercentFontSize > 0
      ) {
        defaultLineHeightPt =
          defaultFontSize * ((node.style as any).lineHeightPercentFontSize / 100);
      }
    }

    const defaultLetterSpacingPt =
      typeof node.style?.letterSpacing === "number" && node.style.letterSpacing !== 0
        ? node.style.letterSpacing * scale
        : undefined;

    // Text color from fills (or style.fills fallback in simplified fixtures)
    let defaultColor = "#000000";
    const textFills =
      node.fills && node.fills.length > 0
        ? node.fills
        : ((node.style as any)?.fills as any[] | undefined);
    if (textFills && textFills.length > 0) {
      const solid = textFills.find(
        (f) => f.type === "SOLID" && f.visible !== false
      );
      if (solid && solid.color) {
        defaultColor = this.figmaColorToHex(solid.color);
      }
    }

    const runs: CanvasTextRun[] = [];

    // Check for characterStyleOverrides
    const overrides = node.characterStyleOverrides;
    const styleTable = node.styleOverrideTable;

    if (
      overrides &&
      Array.isArray(overrides) &&
      overrides.length > 0 &&
      styleTable
    ) {
      let currentRunText = "";
      let currentStyleKey: string | number = overrides[0] ?? 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < characters.length; i++) {
        const char = characters[i];
        const styleKey: string | number = overrides[i] ?? 0;

        if (styleKey === currentStyleKey) {
          currentRunText += char;
        } else {
          if (currentRunText) {
            runs.push(
              this.createRun(
                currentRunText,
                currentStyleKey,
                styleTable,
                defaultFontFamily,
                defaultFontSize,
                defaultFontWeight,
                defaultItalic,
                defaultUnderline,
                defaultStrike,
                defaultColor,
                defaultLineHeightPt,
                defaultLetterSpacingPt,
                scale
              )
            );
          }
          currentRunText = char;
          currentStyleKey = styleKey;
        }
      }

      if (currentRunText) {
        runs.push(
          this.createRun(
            currentRunText,
            currentStyleKey,
            styleTable,
            defaultFontFamily,
            defaultFontSize,
            defaultFontWeight,
            defaultItalic,
            defaultUnderline,
            defaultStrike,
            defaultColor,
            defaultLineHeightPt,
            defaultLetterSpacingPt,
            scale
          )
        );
      }
    } else {
      // Single run
      runs.push({
        text: characters,
        fontFamily: defaultFontFamily,
        fontSizePt: defaultFontSize,
        fontWeight: defaultFontWeight,
        italic: defaultItalic,
        underline: defaultUnderline,
        strike: defaultStrike,
        color: defaultColor,
        lineHeightPt: defaultLineHeightPt,
        letterSpacingPt: defaultLetterSpacingPt,
      });
    }

    return runs;
  }

  private createRun(
    text: string,
    styleKey: number | string,
    styleTable: Record<string | number, any>,
    defaultFamily: string,
    defaultSize: number,
    defaultWeight: number,
    defaultItalic: boolean,
    defaultUnderline: boolean,
    defaultStrike: boolean,
    defaultColor: string,
    defaultLineHeightPt: number | undefined,
    defaultLetterSpacingPt: number | undefined,
    scale: number
  ): CanvasTextRun {
    const override = styleTable[String(styleKey)];
    if (!override) {
      return {
        text,
        fontFamily: defaultFamily,
        fontSizePt: defaultSize,
        fontWeight: defaultWeight,
        italic: defaultItalic,
        underline: defaultUnderline,
        strike: defaultStrike,
        color: defaultColor,
        lineHeightPt: defaultLineHeightPt,
        letterSpacingPt: defaultLetterSpacingPt,
      };
    }

    let color = defaultColor;
    if (override.fills && override.fills.length > 0) {
      const solid = override.fills.find(
        (f: FigmaPaint) => f.type === "SOLID" && f.visible !== false
      );
      if (solid && solid.color) {
        color = this.figmaColorToHex(solid.color);
      }
    }

    const rawWeight = override.fontWeight;
    const resolvedWeight =
      typeof rawWeight === "number"
        ? rawWeight
        : rawWeight
        ? parseInt(String(rawWeight), 10) || defaultWeight
        : defaultWeight;

    let lineHeightPt = defaultLineHeightPt;
    if (override.lineHeightUnit !== "INTRINSIC_%") {
      if (typeof override.lineHeightPx === "number" && override.lineHeightPx > 0) {
        lineHeightPt = override.lineHeightPx * scale;
      }
    }

    const letterSpacingPt =
      typeof override.letterSpacing === "number" && override.letterSpacing !== 0
        ? override.letterSpacing * scale
        : defaultLetterSpacingPt;

    return {
      text,
      fontFamily: override.fontFamily || defaultFamily,
      fontSizePt: override.fontSize ? override.fontSize * scale : defaultSize,
      fontWeight: resolvedWeight,
      italic:
        override.italic !== undefined
          ? override.italic
          : override.fontPostScriptName?.toLowerCase().includes("italic") ||
            defaultItalic,
      underline:
        override.textDecoration === "UNDERLINE" ? true : defaultUnderline,
      strike:
        override.textDecoration === "STRIKETHROUGH" ? true : defaultStrike,
      color,
      lineHeightPt,
      letterSpacingPt,
    };
  }

  private extractTextAlign(node: FigmaNode): "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify" {
    const align = node.style?.textAlignHorizontal;
    if (align === "CENTER") return "center";
    if (align === "RIGHT") return "right";
    if (align === "JUSTIFIED") return "justify";
    return "left";
  }

  private figmaColorToHex(c: FigmaColor): string {
    const r = Math.round((c.r ?? 0) * 255);
    const g = Math.round((c.g ?? 0) * 255);
    const b = Math.round((c.b ?? 0) * 255);
    return (
      "#" +
      [r, g, b]
        .map((x) => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join("")
        .toUpperCase()
    );
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/parser/docxCanvasParser.ts

import JSZip from "jszip";
import {
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasPage,
  CanvasElement,
  CanvasElementType,
  CanvasTextRun,
  CanvasResource,
  CanvasStyle,
  CanvasPadding,
} from "../types.js";
import { SvgPath, vmlToSvgPath } from "../geometry/svgPathParser.js";

/**
 * Parses XML attributes of an opening or self-closing XML tag into a key-value record,
 * regardless of attribute order or whitespace.
 */
export function parseXmlAttributes(tagStr: string): Record<string, string> {
  const attrs: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!tagStr) return attrs;
  const attrRegex = /([a-zA-Z0-9_:-]+)\s*=\s*(["'])([\s\S]*?)\2/g;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
  while ((match = attrRegex.exec(tagStr)) !== null) {
    attrs[match[1]] = match[3];
  }
  return attrs;
}

function parseVmlStyle(styleStr: string): Record<string, string> {
  const result: Record<string, string> = {};
  if (!styleStr) return result;
  for (const part of styleStr.split(";")) {
    const idx = part.indexOf(":");
    if (idx !== -1) {
      const k = part.substring(0, idx).trim().toLowerCase();
      const v = part.substring(idx + 1).trim();
      result[k] = v;
    }
  }
  return result;
}

function parsePt(ptStr?: string): number {
  if (!ptStr) return 0;
  const num = parseFloat(ptStr.replace("pt", "").trim());
  return isNaN(num) ? 0 : num;
}

function parseCoordPair(coordStr?: string): [number, number] {
  if (!coordStr) return [0, 0];
  const parts = coordStr.split(",");
  const x = parsePt(parts[0]);
  const y = parsePt(parts[1]);
  return [x, y];
}

export class DocxCanvasParser {
  public async parse(input: Buffer | Uint8Array | ArrayBuffer | string): Promise<CanvasDocument> {
    let buffer: Buffer | Uint8Array | ArrayBuffer;
    const MAX_INPUT_SIZE = 100 * 1024 * 1024; // 100 MB

    if (typeof input === "string") {
      if (input.includes("\0")) {
        throw new Error("Invalid DOCX path: Null byte detected.");
      }
      const fs = await import("fs");
      const stat = await fs.promises.stat(input);
      if (stat.size > MAX_INPUT_SIZE) {
        throw new Error(`DOCX file exceeds maximum allowed size of 100MB: ${stat.size} bytes`);
      }
      buffer = await fs.promises.readFile(input);
    } else {
      buffer = input;
      const byteLength = "byteLength" in buffer ? buffer.byteLength : (buffer as any).length;
      if (byteLength > MAX_INPUT_SIZE) {
        throw new Error(`DOCX buffer exceeds maximum allowed size of 100MB: ${byteLength} bytes`);
      }
    }

    // ZIP Bomb & entry count protection
    const MAX_ZIP_ENTRIES = 5000;
    const MAX_TOTAL_UNCOMPRESSED_SIZE = 250 * 1024 * 1024; // 250 MB

    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buffer);
    const entries = Object.keys(zip.files);
    if (entries.length > MAX_ZIP_ENTRIES) {
      throw new Error(`DOCX package contains excessive entry count (${entries.length} > ${MAX_ZIP_ENTRIES}). Possible ZIP bomb.`);
    }

    let totalDecompressedBytes = 0;
    for (const filename of entries) {
      const fileEntry = zip.files[filename];
      if (fileEntry && (fileEntry as any)._data && typeof (fileEntry as any)._data.uncompressedSize === "number") {
        totalDecompressedBytes += (fileEntry as any)._data.uncompressedSize;
        if (totalDecompressedBytes > MAX_TOTAL_UNCOMPRESSED_SIZE) {
          throw new Error(`DOCX package uncompressed size exceeds safety limit of 250MB. Possible ZIP bomb.`);
        }
      }
    }

    const docXmlFile = zip.file("word/document.xml");
    if (!docXmlFile) {
      throw new Error("Invalid DOCX package: word/document.xml missing.");
    }

    const docXml = await docXmlFile.async("string");
    const warnings: string[] = [];
    const notices: string[] = [];

    // Diagnostic Scope Checks (F-14):
    if (docXml.includes("<w:tbl")) {
      warnings.push("[SCOPE_NOTICE] Document contains native Word tables (<w:tbl>). Canvas 2D engine targets absolute canvas layouts; flow tables are preserved only as background/container cards.");
    }
    if (docXml.includes("<c:chart") || (docXml.includes("<w:drawing") && docXml.includes("chart"))) {
      warnings.push("[SCOPE_NOTICE] Document contains embedded ChartML (<c:chart>). Embedded office charts are outside Canvas IR shape scope.");
    }
    if (docXml.includes("<w:object") || docXml.includes("<o:OLEObject")) {
      warnings.push("[SCOPE_NOTICE] Document contains embedded OLE objects. OLE binary packages are outside Canvas IR scope.");
    }

    // 1. Load relationships for images with attribute-order agnostic XML parsing
    const relsMap = new Map<string, string>(); // rId -> targetPath
    const relsFile = zip.file("word/_rels/document.xml.rels");
    if (relsFile) {
      const relsXml = await relsFile.async("string");
      const relTagRegex = /<Relationship\b[\s\S]*?>/g;
      let rMatch: RegExpExecArray | null;
      while ((rMatch = relTagRegex.exec(relsXml)) !== null) {
        const attrs = parseXmlAttributes(rMatch[0]);
        if (attrs.Id && attrs.Target) {
          // Security & Scope: Skip external relationships
          if (attrs.TargetMode === "External") {
            notices.push(`Skipped external relationship: ${attrs.Id} -> ${attrs.Target}`);
            continue;
          }
          // Sanitize target path against directory traversal
          const cleanTarget = attrs.Target
            .replace(/\\/g, "/")
            .replace(/^(\.\.\/)+/, "")
            .replace(/^(\.\/)+/, "");
          relsMap.set(attrs.Id, cleanTarget);
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Load media resources
    const resources: Record<string, CanvasResource> = {};
    for (const [rId, target] of relsMap.entries()) {
      if (target.includes("media/")) {
        const zipPath = target.startsWith("word/") ? target : `word/${target}`;
        const imgFile = zip.file(zipPath);
        if (imgFile) {
          const imgData = await imgFile.async("uint8array");
          const resId = target.split("/").pop() || target;
          const extIdx = resId.lastIndexOf(".");
          const ext = extIdx !== -1 ? resId.substring(extIdx).toLowerCase() : "";
          let mimeType = "image/png";
          if (ext === ".jpg" || ext === ".jpeg") mimeType = "image/jpeg";
          else if (ext === ".gif") mimeType = "image/gif";
          else if (ext === ".svg") mimeType = "image/svg+xml";

          resources[resId] = {
            id: resId,
            name: resId,
            mimeType,
            data: imgData,
          };
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Split paragraphs in w:body using depth tracking to avoid inner textbox paragraphs
    const pages: CanvasPage[] = [];
    const bodyMatch = docXml.match(/<w:body[\s>]([\s\S]*?)<\/w:body>/);
    const bodyContent = bodyMatch ? bodyMatch[1] : docXml;

    // Check for unsupported elements in DOCX body (Scope Definition)
    if (bodyContent.includes("<w:tbl")) {
      warnings.push("Native Word table (<w:tbl>) detected in DOCX body. VML Canvas engine specializes in absolute positioned canvas layouts; native flow tables are not parsed into canvas elements.");
    }
    if (bodyContent.includes("<w:drawing") || bodyContent.includes("<wp:anchor") || bodyContent.includes("<wp:inline")) {
      warnings.push("DrawingML (<w:drawing>) detected in DOCX body. VML Canvas engine specializes in absolute VML shapes (<v:*>); DrawingML shapes were skipped.");
    }

    const paragraphs: string[] = [];
    let pDepth = 0;
    let pStartIndex = -1;
    const tagRegex = /<\/?w:p(?:\s[^>]*?)?(\/?)>/g;
    let tMatch: RegExpExecArray | null;

    while ((tMatch = tagRegex.exec(bodyContent)) !== null) {
      const fullTag = tMatch[0];
      const isSelfClosing = fullTag.endsWith("/>") || tMatch[1] === "/";
      const isClosing = fullTag.startsWith("</");

      if (!isClosing && !isSelfClosing) {
        if (pDepth === 0) {
          pStartIndex = tMatch.index;
        }
        pDepth++;
      } else if (isClosing) {
        pDepth--;
        if (pDepth === 0 && pStartIndex !== -1) {
          paragraphs.push(
            bodyContent.substring(pStartIndex, tMatch.index + fullTag.length)
          );
          pStartIndex = -1;
        }
      } else if (isSelfClosing && pDepth === 0) {
        paragraphs.push(tMatch[0]);
      }
    }

    // Extract body-level final <w:sectPr> default page size
    let defaultPageWidthPt = 595.28;
    let defaultPageHeightPt = 841.89;
    const allSectPrMatches = Array.from(bodyContent.matchAll(/<w:sectPr\b[\s\S]*?<\/w:sectPr>/g));
    if (allSectPrMatches.length > 0) {
      const lastSectXml = allSectPrMatches[allSectPrMatches.length - 1][0];
      const pgSzMatch = lastSectXml.match(/<w:pgSz\b[\s\S]*?>/);
      if (pgSzMatch) {
        const pgAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(pgSzMatch[0]);
        const wTwips = parseInt(pgAttrs["w:w"] || "0", 10);
        const hTwips = parseInt(pgAttrs["w:h"] || "0", 10);
        if (wTwips > 0 && hTwips > 0) {
          defaultPageWidthPt = Math.round((wTwips / 20) * 100) / 100;
          defaultPageHeightPt = Math.round((hTwips / 20) * 100) / 100;
        }
      }
    }

    let pageNumber = 1;
    let currentElements: CanvasElement[] = [];
    let currentBgColor: string | undefined;
    let currentSectionSize: { width: number; height: number } | undefined;

    const flushPage = (overrideSize?: { width: number; height: number }) => {
      if (currentElements.length > 0 || currentBgColor || pages.length === 0) {
        const sz = overrideSize || currentSectionSize || {
          width: defaultPageWidthPt,
          height: defaultPageHeightPt,
        };
        pages.push({
          pageNumber,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: sz.width, height: sz.height },
          backgroundColor: currentBgColor,
          elements: [...currentElements].sort((a, b) => a.zIndex - b.zIndex),
        });
        currentElements = [];
        currentBgColor = undefined;
        currentSectionSize = undefined;
        pageNumber++;
      }
    };

    for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < paragraphs.length; pIdx++) {
      const pStr = paragraphs[pIdx];

      // Strip inner <v:textbox> content when checking top-level paragraph properties
      const pTopLevel = pStr.replace(/<v:textbox\b[\s\S]*?<\/v:textbox>/g, "");

      // Check if this paragraph starts a new page
      const hasPageBreak =
        pTopLevel.includes("<w:pageBreakBefore/>") ||
        (pTopLevel.includes("<w:pageBreakBefore") &&
          !pTopLevel.includes('<w:pageBreakBefore w:val="0"'));
      if (hasPageBreak && pIdx > 0) {
        flushPage();
      }

      // Check for raw top-level body text outside of <w:pict>
      const hasPict = pStr.includes("<w:pict");
      const hasDirectText = pTopLevel.includes("<w:t>") || pTopLevel.includes("<w:t ");
      if (hasDirectText && !hasPict) {
        warnings.push(`Page ${pageNumber}: Top-level paragraph contains flow text outside <w:pict>. Only VML canvas textboxes are converted into canvas elements.`);
      }

      // Extract all pict shapes inside this paragraph (self-closing or paired tags)
      const shapeRegex =
        /<v:(rect|roundrect|shape|line|oval|polyline)\b[^>]*\/>|<v:(rect|roundrect|shape|line|oval|polyline)\b[\s\S]*?<\/v:\2>/g;
      const shapes = Array.from(pStr.matchAll(shapeRegex)).map((m) => m[0]);

      for (const sStr of shapes) {
        // Recover background color from full-page background rects (id="bg_N") and skip adding as normal element
        if (sStr.includes('id="bg_')) {
          const bgAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(sStr);
          if (bgAttrs["fillcolor"] && bgAttrs["filled"] !== "f") {
            currentBgColor = bgAttrs["fillcolor"];
          }
          continue;
        }

        const elem = this.parseVmlShape(sStr, relsMap, resources, pageNumber, currentElements.length);
        if (elem) {
          currentElements.push(elem);
        }
      }

      // Check if this paragraph ends a section with an inline <w:sectPr>
      const inlineSectMatch = pTopLevel.match(/<w:sectPr\b[\s\S]*?<\/w:sectPr>/);
      if (inlineSectMatch) {
        const pgSzMatch = inlineSectMatch[0].match(/<w:pgSz\b[\s\S]*?>/);
        let inlineSize: { width: number; height: number } | undefined;
        if (pgSzMatch) {
          const pgAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(pgSzMatch[0]);
          const wTwips = parseInt(pgAttrs["w:w"] || "0", 10);
          const hTwips = parseInt(pgAttrs["w:h"] || "0", 10);
          if (wTwips > 0 && hTwips > 0) {
            inlineSize = {
              width: Math.round((wTwips / 20) * 100) / 100,
              height: Math.round((hTwips / 20) * 100) / 100,
            };
          }
        }
        if (pIdx < paragraphs.length - 1) {
          flushPage(inlineSize);
        } else if (inlineSize) {
          currentSectionSize = inlineSize;
        }
      }
    }

    // Flush last page
    flushPage();

    const firstPage = pages[0];
    const docWidthPt = firstPage ? firstPage.bounds.width : defaultPageWidthPt;
    const docHeightPt = firstPage ? firstPage.bounds.height : defaultPageHeightPt;
    const docOrientation = docWidthPt > docHeightPt ? "landscape" : "portrait";

    return {
      title: "Extracted Canvas Document",
      pageSize: {
        name:
          (Math.abs(docWidthPt - 595.28) < 2 && Math.abs(docHeightPt - 841.89) < 2) ||
          (Math.abs(docWidthPt - 841.89) < 2 && Math.abs(docHeightPt - 595.28) < 2)
            ? "A4"
            : "Custom",
        widthPt: docWidthPt,
        heightPt: docHeightPt,
        orientation: docOrientation,
        unit: "pt",
      },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages,
      resources,
      diagnostics: { notices, warnings },
    };
  }

  private parseVmlShape(
    shapeXml: string,
    relsMap: Map<string, string>,
    resources: Record<string, CanvasResource>,
    pageIdx: number,
    elemIdx: number
  ): CanvasElement | null {
    const tagMatch = shapeXml.match(/<v:(rect|roundrect|shape|line|oval|polyline)\b/);
    if (!tagMatch) return null;
    const tag = tagMatch[1];

    const openTagMatch = shapeXml.match(/<v:[a-zA-Z0-9_:-]+[^>]*>/);
    const openTagStr = openTagMatch ? openTagMatch[0] : shapeXml;
    const attrs = parseXmlAttributes(openTagStr);
    const styleStr = attrs["style"] || "";
    const styleMap = parseVmlStyle(styleStr);

    // Extract metadata & original IDs preserved in alt / o:title / id
    const altText = attrs["alt"];
    const titleText = attrs["o:title"];
    const elementId = altText || attrs["id"] || `elem_p${pageIdx}_e${elemIdx}`;
    const elementName = titleText || attrs["name"] || undefined;

    const zIndex = parseInt(styleMap["z-index"] || "1", 10);
    const strokeDashed =
      shapeXml.includes('dashstyle="dash"') || shapeXml.includes('dashstyle="dot"') || undefined;

    // 1. Line shape (<v:line from="..." to="..."/>)
    if (tag === "line") {
      const [x1, y1] = parseCoordPair(attrs["from"]);
      const [x2, y2] = parseCoordPair(attrs["to"]);

      const x = Math.min(x1, x2);
      const y = Math.min(y1, y2);
      const width = Math.abs(x2 - x1);
      const height = Math.abs(y2 - y1);

      const strokeColor = attrs["strokecolor"] ? attrs["strokecolor"] : undefined;
      const strokeWidth = attrs["strokeweight"] ? parsePt(attrs["strokeweight"]) : 1;

      return {
        id: elementId,
        name: elementName || "Line",
        type: "line",
        bounds: { x, y, width, height },
        zIndex,
        style: {
          strokeColor,
          strokeWidth,
          strokeDashed,
        },
      };
    }

    // Coordinates for Rect / Shape / Roundrect
    const x = parsePt(styleMap["margin-left"]);
    const y = parsePt(styleMap["margin-top"]);
    const width = parsePt(styleMap["width"]);
    const height = parsePt(styleMap["height"]);

    const filled = attrs["filled"] !== "f" && attrs["filled"] !== "false";
    const stroked = attrs["stroked"] !== "f" && attrs["stroked"] !== "false";

    const fillColor = filled && attrs["fillcolor"] ? attrs["fillcolor"] : undefined;
    const strokeColor = stroked && attrs["strokecolor"] ? attrs["strokecolor"] : undefined;
    const strokeWidth = stroked && attrs["strokeweight"] ? parsePt(attrs["strokeweight"]) : undefined;

    let cornerRadius: number | undefined;
    if (tag === "roundrect" && attrs["arcsize"]) {
      const arc = parseFloat(attrs["arcsize"]);
      if (!isNaN(arc) && arc > 0) {
        const minSide = Math.max(1, Math.min(width, height));
        cornerRadius = Math.round(arc * minSide * 100) / 100;
      }
    }

    const style: CanvasStyle = {
      fillColor,
      strokeColor,
      strokeWidth,
      strokeDashed,
      cornerRadius,
    };

    // 2. Check imagedata
    const imgTagMatch = shapeXml.match(/<v:imagedata\b[\s\S]*?>/);
    if (imgTagMatch) {
      const imgAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(imgTagMatch[0]);
      const rId = imgAttrs["r:id"] || imgAttrs["id"];
      if (rId) {
        const target = relsMap.get(rId);
        const resId = target ? (target.split("/").pop() || target) : rId;

        const parseVmlCrop = (val?: string): number => {
          if (!val) return 0;
          if (val.endsWith("f")) {
            const num = parseFloat(val.slice(0, -1));
            return isNaN(num) ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, num / 65536));
          }
          const num = parseFloat(val);
          return isNaN(num) ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, num));
        };

        const cTop = parseVmlCrop(imgAttrs["croptop"]);
        const cBottom = parseVmlCrop(imgAttrs["cropbottom"]);
        const cLeft = parseVmlCrop(imgAttrs["cropleft"]);
        const cRight = parseVmlCrop(imgAttrs["cropright"]);
        const hasCrop = cTop > 0 || cBottom > 0 || cLeft > 0 || cRight > 0;

        return {
          id: elementId,
          name: elementName || imgAttrs["o:title"] || "image",
          type: "image",
          bounds: { x, y, width, height },
          zIndex,
          style,
          imageRef: resId,
          imageScaleMode: hasCrop ? "CROP" : "FILL",
          imageCrop: hasCrop ? { top: cTop, bottom: cBottom, left: cLeft, right: cRight } : undefined,
        };
      }
    }

    // 3. Check textbox, padding & vertical alignment
    let innerPaddingPt: CanvasPadding | undefined;
    let verticalAlign: "top" | "center" | "bottom" | undefined;
    const textboxTagMatch = shapeXml.match(/<v:textbox\b[\s\S]*?>/);
    if (textboxTagMatch) {
      const tbAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(textboxTagMatch[0]);
      if (tbAttrs["inset"]) {
        const parts = tbAttrs["inset"].split(",").map((p) => parsePt(p));
        if (parts.length >= 4) {
          innerPaddingPt = {
            left: parts[0],
            top: parts[1],
            right: parts[2],
            bottom: parts[3],
          };
        }
      }
      if (tbAttrs["style"]) {
        const tbStyle = parseVmlStyle(tbAttrs["style"]);
        const anchor = tbStyle["v-text-anchor"];
        if (anchor === "middle") verticalAlign = "center";
        else if (anchor === "bottom") verticalAlign = "bottom";
        else if (anchor === "top") verticalAlign = "top";
      }
    }

    const textRuns: CanvasTextRun[] = [];
    let align: "left" | "center" | "right" | "justify" = "left";

    const jcTagMatch = shapeXml.match(/<w:jc\b[\s\S]*?>/);
    if (jcTagMatch) {
      const jcAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(jcTagMatch[0]);
      const jc = jcAttrs["w:val"];
      if (jc === "center" || jc === "right" || jc === "justify") {
        align = jc;
      }
    }

    // Check paragraph line spacing inside <w:pPr>
    let lineHeightPt: number | undefined;
    const pPrMatch = shapeXml.match(/<w:pPr\b[\s\S]*?<\/w:pPr>/);
    const spacingTagMatch = (pPrMatch ? pPrMatch[0] : shapeXml).match(/<w:spacing\b[^>]*w:line=[^>]*>/);
    if (spacingTagMatch) {
      const spAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(spacingTagMatch[0]);
      if (spAttrs["w:line"] && spAttrs["w:lineRule"] === "exact") {
        lineHeightPt = parseInt(spAttrs["w:line"], 10) / 20;
      }
    }

    const pRegex = /<w:p[\s>][\s\S]*?<\/w:p>/g;
    const paragraphs = Array.from(shapeXml.matchAll(pRegex)).map((m) => m[0]);
    const parasToProcess = paragraphs.length > 0 ? paragraphs : [shapeXml];

    for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < parasToProcess.length; pIdx++) {
      const pXml = parasToProcess[pIdx];
      if (pIdx > 0 && textRuns.length > 0) {
        const lastRun = textRuns[textRuns.length - 1];
        if (!lastRun.text.endsWith("\n")) {
          lastRun.text += "\n";
        }
      }

      const rRegex = /<w:r[\s>][\s\S]*?<\/w:r>/g;
      const runs = Array.from(pXml.matchAll(rRegex)).map((m) => m[0]);

      for (const rXml of runs) {
        const tokenRegex = /<w:t[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/w:t>|<w:br\b[^>]*\/?>|<w:cr\b[^>]*\/?>/g;
        let text = "";
        let tokenMatch: RegExpExecArray | null;
        let hasContent = false;
        while ((tokenMatch = tokenRegex.exec(rXml)) !== null) {
          hasContent = true;
          if (tokenMatch[1] !== undefined) {
            text += tokenMatch[1]
              .replace(/&amp;/g, "&")
              .replace(/&lt;/g, "<")
              .replace(/&gt;/g, ">")
              .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
              .replace(/&apos;/g, "'");
          } else {
            text += "\n";
          }
        }

        if (hasContent && text.length > 0) {
          const rFontsMatch = rXml.match(/<w:rFonts\b[\s\S]*?>/);
          let fontFamily = "Pretendard";
          if (rFontsMatch) {
            const fAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(rFontsMatch[0]);
            fontFamily = fAttrs["w:eastAsia"] || fAttrs["w:ascii"] || "Pretendard";
          }

          const szMatch = rXml.match(/<w:sz\b[\s\S]*?>/);
          let fontSizePt = 10;
          if (szMatch) {
            const szAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(szMatch[0]);
            if (szAttrs["w:val"]) {
              fontSizePt = parseInt(szAttrs["w:val"], 10) / 2;
            }
          }

          const colorMatch = rXml.match(/<w:color\b[\s\S]*?>/);
          let color = "#000000";
          if (colorMatch) {
            const cAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(colorMatch[0]);
            if (cAttrs["w:val"]) {
              color = `#${cAttrs["w:val"]}`;
            }
          }

          let letterSpacingPt: number | undefined;
          const rSpMatch = rXml.match(/<w:spacing\b[^>]*w:val=[^>]*>/);
          if (rSpMatch) {
            const rSpAttrs = parseXmlAttributes(rSpMatch[0]);
            if (rSpAttrs["w:val"]) {
              const twips = parseInt(rSpAttrs["w:val"], 10);
              if (!isNaN(twips)) {
                letterSpacingPt = Math.round((twips / 20) * 100) / 100;
              }
            }
          }

          const bold = rXml.includes("<w:b/>") || rXml.includes("<w:b ");
          const italic = rXml.includes("<w:i/>") || rXml.includes("<w:i ");
          const underline = rXml.includes("<w:u ") || rXml.includes("<w:u/>");
          const strike = rXml.includes("<w:strike ") || rXml.includes("<w:strike/>");

          textRuns.push({
            text,
            fontFamily,
            fontSizePt,
            fontWeight: bold ? 700 : 400,
            italic: italic || undefined,
            underline: underline || undefined,
            strike: strike || undefined,
            color,
            lineHeightPt,
            letterSpacingPt,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    const hasText = textRuns.length > 0;
    let type: CanvasElementType = hasText ? "text" : "card";
    if (tag === "oval") {
      type = "oval";
    } else if (tag === "shape" && attrs["path"]) {
      type = "vector";
    }

    let vectorPoints: { x: number; y: number }[] | undefined;
    let vectorSubpaths: { points: { x: number; y: number }[]; closed: boolean }[] | undefined;
    let vectorPath: string | undefined;
    if (type === "vector" && attrs["path"]) {
      vectorPath = attrs["path"];
      const cleanPath = vmlToSvgPath(vectorPath);
      const parsed = SvgPath.parse(cleanPath);
      const wHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(width * 100));
      const hHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(height * 100));
      vectorPoints = parsed.toHwpxPolygonPoints(wHwpu, hHwpu);
      vectorSubpaths = parsed.toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(wHwpu, hHwpu);
    }

    return {
      id: elementId,
      name: elementName,
      type,
      bounds: { x, y, width, height },
      zIndex,
      style,
      innerPaddingPt,
      textRuns: hasText ? textRuns : undefined,
      paragraphAlign: align,
      verticalAlign,
      vectorPath,
      vectorPoints,
      vectorSubpaths,
    };
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.ts
================================================================================
import JSZip from "jszip";
import {
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasPage,
  CanvasElement,
  CanvasTextRun,
  CanvasResource,
  ptToTwip,
} from "../types.js";

export interface DocxCanvasRendererOptions {
  creator?: string;
}

function escapeXml(str: string): string {
  if (!str) return "";
  return str
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&apos;")
    .replace(/[\x00-\x08\x0B\x0C\x0E-\x1F]/g, "");
}

function cleanHex(color?: string, fallback: string = "000000"): string {
  if (!color) return fallback;
  return color.replace("#", "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

export function resolveEastAsiaFont(fontFamily?: string): { ascii: string; eastAsia: string } {
  if (!fontFamily) {
    return { ascii: "Pretendard", eastAsia: "맑은 고딕" };
  }
  const f = fontFamily.trim();
  const isKoreanFont =
    f.includes("맑은 고딕") ||
    f.includes("Malgun") ||
    f.includes("Pretendard") ||
    f.includes("Noto Sans KR") ||
    f.includes("Nanum") ||
    f.includes("나눔") ||
    f.includes("돋움") ||
    f.includes("Dotum") ||
    f.includes("굴림") ||
    f.includes("Gulim") ||
    f.includes("바탕") ||
    f.includes("Batang") ||
    f.includes("Gungsuh") ||
    f.includes("KoPub");

  if (isKoreanFont) {
    return { ascii: f, eastAsia: f };
  }
  return { ascii: f, eastAsia: "맑은 고딕" };
}

export class DocxCanvasRenderer {
  private creator: string;

  constructor(options?: DocxCanvasRendererOptions) {
    this.creator = options?.creator || "Figma2Word Canvas Engine";
  }

  public async buildZip(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<JSZip> {
    const zip = new JSZip();

    // 1. [Content_Types].xml
    zip.file("[Content_Types].xml", this.buildContentTypesXml(doc));

    // 2. _rels/.rels
    zip.file("_rels/.rels", this.buildRootRelsXml());

    // 3. docProps/core.xml & app.xml
    zip.file("docProps/core.xml", this.buildCorePropsXml(doc));
    zip.file("docProps/app.xml", this.buildAppPropsXml(doc));

    // 4. word/styles.xml & settings.xml
    zip.file("word/styles.xml", this.buildStylesXml());
    zip.file("word/settings.xml", this.buildSettingsXml());

    // 5. word/media/
    const imageRelMap = new Map<string, string>(); // resourceId -> rId
    let rIdCounter = 2; // rId1 is styles.xml

    for (const [resId, res] of Object.entries(doc.resources)) {
      const rId = `rId${rIdCounter++}`;
      imageRelMap.set(resId, rId);
      zip.file(`word/media/${resId}`, res.data);
    }

    // 6. word/_rels/document.xml.rels
    zip.file(
      "word/_rels/document.xml.rels",
      this.buildDocumentRelsXml(imageRelMap)
    );

    // 7. word/document.xml
    zip.file("word/document.xml", this.buildDocumentXml(doc, imageRelMap));

    return zip;
  }

  public async renderToUint8Array(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    const zip = await this.buildZip(doc);
    return await zip.generateAsync({
      type: "uint8array",
      compression: "DEFLATE",
      compressionOptions: { level: 6 },
    });
  }

  public async renderToBlob(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Blob> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return new Blob([uint8 as any], {
      type: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document",
    });
  }

  public async renderToBuffer(doc: CanvasDocument): Promise<Buffer> {
    const uint8 = await this.renderToUint8Array(doc);
    return Buffer.from(uint8.buffer, uint8.byteOffset, uint8.byteLength);
  }

  private buildContentTypesXml(doc: CanvasDocument): string {
    const mediaExtensions = new Set<string>(["png", "jpeg", "jpg"]);
    let defaultEntries = "";
    for (const ext of mediaExtensions) {
      const mime = ext === "png" ? "image/png" : "image/jpeg";
      defaultEntries += `<Default Extension="${ext}" ContentType="${mime}"/>`;
    }

    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">
  <Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>
  <Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/>
  ${defaultEntries}
  <Override PartName="/word/document.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/word/styles.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.styles+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/word/settings.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.settings+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/docProps/core.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.core-properties+xml"/>
  <Override PartName="/docProps/app.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.extended-properties+xml"/>
</Types>`.trim();
  }

  private buildRootRelsXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">
  <Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="word/document.xml"/>
  <Relationship Id="rId2" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships/metadata/core-properties" Target="docProps/core.xml"/>
  <Relationship Id="rId3" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/extended-properties" Target="docProps/app.xml"/>
</Relationships>`.trim();
  }

  private buildCorePropsXml(doc: CanvasDocument): string {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<cp:coreProperties xmlns:cp="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/metadata/core-properties"
                   xmlns:dc="http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/"
                   xmlns:dcterms="http://purl.org/dc/terms/"
                   xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance">
  <dc:title>${escapeXml(doc.title)}</dc:title>
  <dc:creator>${escapeXml(this.creator)}</dc:creator>
  <cp:lastModifiedBy>${escapeXml(this.creator)}</cp:lastModifiedBy>
  <dcterms:created xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">${now}</dcterms:created>
  <dcterms:modified xsi:type="dcterms:W3CDTF">${now}</dcterms:modified>
</cp:coreProperties>`.trim();
  }

  private buildAppPropsXml(doc: CanvasDocument): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Properties xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/extended-properties">
  <Application>${escapeXml(this.creator)}</Application>
  <Pages>${doc.pages.length}</Pages>
</Properties>`.trim();
  }

  private buildStylesXml(): string {
    return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<w:styles xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main">
  <w:docDefaults>
    <w:rPrDefault>
      <w:rPr>
        <w:rFonts w:ascii="Inter" w:hAnsi="Inter" w:eastAsia="맑은 고딕" w:cs="Inter"/>
        <w:sz w:val="20"/>
        <w:szCs w:val="20"/>
        <w:lang w:val="ko-KR" w:eastAsia="ko-KR" w:bidi="ar-SA"/>
      </w:rPr>
    </w:rPrDefault>
    <w:pPrDefault>
      <w:pPr>
