        backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",

            id: "figma_node_title_1",

            name: "Company Logo",
            type: "image",
            bounds: { x: 40.5, y: 140.0, width: 100.0, height: 100.0 },
            zIndex: 3,
            style: {},
            imageRef: "img-1",
          },
        ],
      },
      {

        name: "Page 2 - Content",
        bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
        backgroundColor: "#F9FAFB",
        elements: [
          {
            id: "figma_node_card_1",
            name: "Data Card",
            type: "card",
            bounds: { x: 40.5, y: 50.0, width: 250.0, height: 150.0 },
            zIndex: 1,
            style: {
              fillColor: "#FFFFFF",
              strokeColor: "#E5E7EB",
              strokeWidth: 1,
              cornerRadius: 8,
            },
            textRuns: [
              {

                color: "#1F2937",
              },

  };

  it("renders sample Canvas IR into a structurally valid DOCX buffer preserving Pretendard & EastAsia fonts", async () => {
    const renderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
    const docxBuf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(sampleCanvasDoc);

    expect(docxBuf).toBeDefined();
    expect(docxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);

    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
    expect(zip.file("[Content_Types].xml")).toBeDefined();
    expect(zip.file("word/document.xml")).toBeDefined();
    expect(zip.file("word/media/image1.png")).toBeDefined();

    const docXml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(docXml).toContain("w:pageBreakBefore");
    expect(docXml).toContain("Executive Business Report");
    expect(docXml).toContain("Quarterly Revenue: $4.2M");
    expect(docXml).toContain("v:textbox");
    expect(docXml).toContain("v:line");
    expect(docXml).toContain('alt="figma_node_title_1"');
    expect(docXml).toContain('alt="figma_node_line_1"');
    expect(docXml).toContain('w:eastAsia="Pretendard"');
    expect(docXml).toContain('w:eastAsia="맑은 고딕"');
    expect(docXml).toContain('inset="10.00pt,5.00pt,10.00pt,5.00pt"');
  });

  it("renders sample Canvas IR into a valid HWPX with 7 OWPML fontface blocks and dynamic charPr fontRef", async () => {
    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const hwpxBuf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(sampleCanvasDoc);

    expect(hwpxBuf).toBeDefined();
    expect(hwpxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);

    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
    expect(zip.file("mimetype")).toBeDefined();
    expect(zip.file("version.xml")).toBeDefined();
    expect(zip.file("Contents/content.hpf")).toBeDefined();
    expect(zip.file("Contents/header.xml")).toBeDefined();
    expect(zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")).toBeDefined();
    expect(zip.file("BinData/BIN0001.png")).toBeDefined();

    // Verify uncompressed mimetype first entry
    const mimeStr = await zip.file("mimetype")!.async("string");
    expect(mimeStr.trim()).toBe("application/hwp+zip");

    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:fontfaces itemCnt="7">');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="HANGUL"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="LATIN"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('face="Pretendard"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('face="맑은 고딕"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain("hh:fontRef");

    const sectionXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(sectionXml).toContain('pageBreak="1"');
    expect(sectionXml).toContain("Executive Business Report");
    expect(sectionXml).toContain("Quarterly Revenue: $4.2M");
    expect(sectionXml).toContain("hp:drawText");
  });

  it("parses generated DOCX back into Canvas IR with 100% data and ID preservation (Roundtrip Zero Loss)", async () => {
    const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
    const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(sampleCanvasDoc);

    const docxParser = new DocxCanvasParser();
    const parsedDoc = await docxParser.parse(docxBuf);

    expect(parsedDoc.pages.length).toBe(2);
    // Page 1: title, line, image (3 elements)
    expect(parsedDoc.pages[0].elements.length).toBe(3);
    // Page 2: card (1 element)
    expect(parsedDoc.pages[1].elements.length).toBe(1);

    const titleEl = parsedDoc.pages[0].elements[0];
    expect(titleEl.id).toBe("figma_node_title_1");
    expect(titleEl.name).toBe("Title Header");
    expect(titleEl.type).toBe("text");
    expect(titleEl.textRuns?.[0].text).toContain("Executive Business Report");
    expect(titleEl.textRuns?.[0].fontFamily).toBe("Pretendard");
    expect(titleEl.bounds.width).toBeCloseTo(514.0, 0);
    expect(titleEl.innerPaddingPt?.left).toBe(10);
    expect(titleEl.innerPaddingPt?.top).toBe(5);

    const lineEl = parsedDoc.pages[0].elements[1];
    expect(lineEl.id).toBe("figma_node_line_1");
    expect(lineEl.name).toBe("Accent Divider");
    expect(lineEl.type).toBe("line");
    expect(lineEl.bounds.width).toBeCloseTo(514.0, 0);

    const imgEl = parsedDoc.pages[0].elements[2];
    expect(imgEl.id).toBe("figma_node_img_1");
    expect(imgEl.type).toBe("image");
    expect(imgEl.imageRef).toBe("img-1");

    const cardEl = parsedDoc.pages[1].elements[0];
    expect(cardEl.id).toBe("figma_node_card_1");
    expect(cardEl.textRuns?.[0].text).toContain("Quarterly Revenue: $4.2M");
    expect(cardEl.textRuns?.[0].fontFamily).toBe("맑은 고딕");
  });

  it("diagnoses and logs warnings when encountering unsupported DOCX elements like DrawingML or Tables", async () => {
    const mockDocxZip = new JSZip();
    const mockDocXml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main"
            xmlns:wp="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/wordprocessingDrawing">
  <w:body>
    <w:p><w:r><w:t>Direct body flow text</w:t></w:r></w:p>
    <w:tbl><w:tr><w:tc><w:p><w:r><w:t>Cell</w:t></w:r></w:p></w:tc></w:tr></w:tbl>
    <w:p><w:drawing><wp:inline/></w:drawing></w:p>
  </w:body>
</w:document>`;

    mockDocxZip.file("word/document.xml", mockDocXml);
    const buf = await mockDocxZip.generateAsync({ type: "nodebuffer" });

    const parser = new DocxCanvasParser();
    const res = await parser.parse(buf);

    expect(res.diagnostics.warnings.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(res.diagnostics.warnings.some((w) => w.includes("<w:tbl>"))).toBe(true);
    expect(res.diagnostics.warnings.some((w) => w.includes("DrawingML"))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("Canvas Pipeline - End to End", () => {
  it("translates sample DOCX to HWPX directly via pipeline", async () => {
    const pipeline = new CanvasPipeline();
    // Render a self-contained test DOCX first to guarantee file presence
    const renderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
    const testDoc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Self Contained E2E Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {},
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "test-title",
              name: "E2E Title",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 400, height: 50 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              textRuns: [{ text: "Pipeline Test Heading", fontFamily: "Pretendard", fontSizePt: 16 }],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const docxBuf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(testDoc);
    const testDocxPath = path.resolve("debug/canvas-audit/test-e2e-input.docx");
    const outHwpxPath = path.resolve("debug/canvas-audit/test-e2e-output.hwpx");
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(testDocxPath), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(testDocxPath, docxBuf);

    const res = await pipeline.convertDocxToHwpx(testDocxPath, {
      outputHwpxPath: outHwpxPath,
    });

    expect(res.stats.pageCount).toBe(1);
    expect(res.stats.elementCount).toBe(1);
    expect(fs.existsSync(outHwpxPath)).toBe(true);

    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(fs.readFileSync(outHwpxPath));
    expect(zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")).toBeDefined();
    const sectionXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(sectionXml).toContain("Pipeline Test Heading");
  });
});

describe("Canvas Engine - Visual Fidelity & Parser Improvements (Phase 13C)", () => {
  it("strips container fill on pure TEXT Figma nodes to prevent opaque background overlay", () => {
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
    const mockFigmaDoc = {
      document: {
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Document",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [
          {
            id: "0:1",
            name: "Page 1",
            type: "CANVAS",
            children: [
              {
                id: "1:1",
                name: "A4 Frame",
                type: "FRAME",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 794, height: 1123 },
                children: [
                  {
                    id: "1:2",
                    name: "Pure Text",
                    type: "TEXT",
                    absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 30 },
                    characters: "Hello World",
                    fills: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.2, g: 0.2, b: 0.2, a: 1 } }],
                    style: {
                      fontFamily: "Pretendard",
                      fontSize: 16,
                      fontWeight: 700,
                    },
                  },
                ],
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
      },
    };

    const canvasDoc = parser.parse(mockFigmaDoc as any);
    expect(canvasDoc.pages.length).toBe(1);
    const textEl = canvasDoc.pages[0].elements.find((e) => e.name === "Pure Text");
    expect(textEl).toBeDefined();
    // Element style fillColor must NOT be set for pure text node
    expect(textEl?.style.fillColor).toBeUndefined();
    // But textRun color should have the font color
    expect(textEl?.textRuns?.[0].color).toBeDefined();
  });

  it("calculates correct VML crop fractions for aspect ratio preservation in DocxCanvasRenderer", async () => {
    const renderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
    // Dummy PNG buffer with 200x100 resolution (aspect ratio 2.0)
    const pngHeader = Buffer.from([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, // PNG sig
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d, // IHDR len 13
      0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52, // "IHDR"
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // width = 200
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // height = 100 (aspect = 2.0)
      0x08, 0x06, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, // CRC dummy
    ]);

    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Crop Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {
        "crop-img": {
          id: "crop-img",
          name: "wide.png",
          mimeType: "image/png",
          data: pngHeader,
        },
      },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "img-crop-el",
              name: "Cropped Image",
              type: "image",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 }, // square box (aspect 1.0) for 2.0 image -> crops horizontally
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              imageRef: "crop-img",
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const docXml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // In 2.0 image placed in 1.0 container, rendered width = 100, rendered height = 100.
    // targetAspect (1.0) < origAspect (2.0): crop horizontally: cropleft & cropright
    expect(docXml).toContain("cropleft=");
    expect(docXml).toContain("cropright=");
  });

  it("parses Figma ELLIPSE into oval CanvasElement and renders native v:oval in DOCX and hp:oval in HWPX", async () => {
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
    const mockDoc = {
      document: {
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Doc",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [
          {
            id: "0:1",
            name: "Page",
            type: "CANVAS",
            children: [
              {
                id: "1:1",
                name: "Frame",
                type: "FRAME",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 794, height: 1123 },
                children: [
                  {
                    id: "1:2",
                    name: "Status Circle",
                    type: "ELLIPSE",
                    absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 100, y: 100, width: 40, height: 40 },
                    fills: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.2, g: 0.6, b: 0.2, a: 1 } }],
                  },
                ],
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
      },
    };

    const canvasDoc = parser.parse(mockDoc as any);
    const ovalEl = canvasDoc.pages[0].elements.find((e) => e.name === "Status Circle");
    expect(ovalEl).toBeDefined();
    expect(ovalEl?.type).toBe("oval");

    // Render DOCX
    const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
    const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDoc);
    const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
    const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(docXml).toContain("<v:oval");
    expect(docXml).toContain("</v:oval>");
    // Textbox should not have mso-wrap-style:none
    expect(docXml).not.toContain("<v:textbox style=\"mso-fit-shape-to-text:f;mso-next-textbox:none;mso-wrap-style:none\"");

    // Render HWPX
    const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDoc);
    const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
    const sectionXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(sectionXml).toContain("<hp:oval");
    expect(sectionXml).toContain("</hp:oval>");
  });

  it("parses Figma VECTOR node with icon context into vector CanvasElement with native VML path", async () => {
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
    const mockDoc = {
      document: {
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Doc",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [
          {
            id: "0:1",
            name: "Page",
            type: "CANVAS",
            children: [
              {
                id: "1:1",
                name: "Frame",
                type: "FRAME",
                absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 794, height: 1123 },
                children: [
                  {
                    id: "1:2",
                    name: "alert-triangle",
                    type: "FRAME",
                    absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 14, height: 14 },
                    children: [
                      {
                        id: "1:3",
                        name: "Vector",
                        type: "VECTOR",
                        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 14, height: 14 },
                        strokes: [{ type: "SOLID", color: { r: 0.9, g: 0.2, b: 0.2, a: 1 } }],
                        strokeWeight: 2,
                      },
                    ],
                  },
                ],
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
      },
    };

    const canvasDoc = parser.parse(mockDoc as any);
    const vecEl = canvasDoc.pages[0].elements.find((e) => e.name === "Vector");
    expect(vecEl).toBeDefined();
    expect(vecEl?.type).toBe("vector");
    expect(vecEl?.vectorPath).toBeDefined();
    expect(vecEl?.vectorPath).toContain("m 500,0");
    expect(vecEl?.vectorPoints).toBeDefined();
    expect(vecEl?.vectorPoints!.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // Verify DOCX VML rendering
    const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
    const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDoc);
    const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
    const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(docXml).toContain("<v:shape");
    expect(docXml).toContain('coordsize="1000,1000"');
    expect(docXml).toContain('path="m 500,0');

    // Verify HWPX OWPML hp:polygon rendering (Problem A resolved)
    const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDoc);
    const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
    const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:polygon");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hc:pt");
    expect(secXml).toContain("</hp:polygon>");
  });

  it("SvgPath correctly parses complex SVG paths with bezier, arcs, and relative commands", async () => {
    const { SvgPath } = await import("../src/canvas/geometry/svgPathParser.js");

    // 1. Line and polygon with relative coords
    const polySvg = "M 10 10 l 50 0 l 0 50 l -50 0 Z";
    const parsedPoly = SvgPath.parse(polySvg);
    expect(parsedPoly.commands.length).toBe(5);
    const vmlPoly = parsedPoly.toVmlPath(1000, 1000);
    expect(vmlPoly).toContain("m 0,0");
    expect(vmlPoly).toContain("l 1000,0");
    expect(vmlPoly).toContain("l 1000,1000");
    expect(vmlPoly).toContain("l 0,1000");
    expect(vmlPoly).toContain("x e");

    // 2. Smooth bezier, quadratic, and arc
    const curveSvg = "M 0 0 C 10 20 30 20 40 0 S 70 -20 80 0 Q 90 20 100 0 A 10 10 0 0 1 120 0 Z";
    const parsedCurve = SvgPath.parse(curveSvg);
    expect(parsedCurve.commands.length).toBeGreaterThan(5);
    const hwpxPts = parsedCurve.toHwpxPolygonPoints(1200, 400, 4);
    expect(hwpxPts.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
    expect(hwpxPts[0].x).toBe(0);

    // 3. Robustness on empty or invalid input
    const emptyParsed = SvgPath.parse("");
    expect(emptyParsed.commands.length).toBe(0);
    expect(emptyParsed.toVmlPath()).toBe("");
    expect(emptyParsed.toHwpxPolygonPoints(100, 100)).toEqual([]);
  });
});



================================================================================
FILE: tests/phase16-1-remediation.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument, CanvasElement } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 16.1 Independent Audit Findings Remediation Tests", () => {
  // =========================================================================
  // 1. P0: Real Figma Input Landscape Handling (complex-nested-dashboard)
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P0: Real Figma Input Landscape Handling", () => {
    it("should dynamically detect landscape orientation and dimensions from top-level Figma frame", async () => {
      const samplePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/synthetic-figma/complex-nested-dashboard.json");
      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(samplePath, "utf-8"));

      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Dashboard Landscape" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      // Frame is 842 x 595 (A4 landscape ratio)
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeGreaterThan(doc.pageSize.heightPt);
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.orientation).toBe("landscape");

      // Verify DOCX Output has landscape orientation and matching twip dimensions
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      const pgSzMatch = docXml.match(/<w:pgSz\b[^>]*\/?>/);
      expect(pgSzMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:orient="landscape"');
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:w="16838"');
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:h="11906"');

      // Verify HWPX Output has WIDELY and matching HWPU dimensions
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const pagePrMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:pagePr\b[^>]*\/?>/);
      expect(pagePrMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('landscape="WIDELY"');
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('width="84189"');
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('height="59528"');
    });

    it("should preserve portrait A4 dimensions for portrait documents (multi-page-report)", () => {
      const samplePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/synthetic-figma/multi-page-report.json");
      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(samplePath, "utf-8"));

      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Multi-page Portrait" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeGreaterThan(doc.pageSize.widthPt);
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.orientation).toBe("portrait");
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. P1: HWPX Multi-Subpath and Open Path Separation
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: HWPX Multi-Subpath and Open Path", () => {
    it("should emit multiple disconnected subpaths as separate HWPX polygons", async () => {
      const compoundDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Compound Subpath Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "vec1",
                name: "Two Boxes",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#00FF00", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1 },
                rawSvgPath: "M 0 0 L 50 0 L 50 50 L 0 50 Z M 100 0 L 150 0 L 150 50 L 100 50 Z",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(compoundDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const polygonMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:polygon\b/g);
      expect(polygonMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(polygonMatches!.length).toBe(2);
    });

    it("should preserve open paths (like checkmarks) without closing lines or spurious fills", async () => {
      const checkmarkDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Checkmark Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "check1",
                name: "Checkmark",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { strokeColor: "#0000FF", strokeWidth: 2 },
                rawSvgPath: "M 10 50 L 40 80 L 90 20",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(checkmarkDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Open paths must NOT be emitted as <hp:polygon> (which auto-closes in Hancom)
      expect(secXml).not.toContain("<hp:polygon");
      const lineMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b/g);
      expect(lineMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatches!.length).toBe(2);
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:pt0 x="0" y="5000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:pt1 x="3750" y="10000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:pt1 x="10000" y="0"/>');
    });

    it("should distinguish closed vs open subpaths in mixed multi-contour paths", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Path Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "mix1",
                name: "Circle and Line",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 200 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#FF0000", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1 },
                // Closed outer box + open inner slash
                rawSvgPath: "M 0 0 L 100 0 L 100 100 L 0 100 Z M 20 20 L 80 80",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Closed box is emitted as <hp:polygon> with fill; open slash is emitted as <hp:line> without fill
      const polygonMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:polygon\b/g);
      expect(polygonMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(polygonMatches!.length).toBe(1);

      const lineMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b/g);
      expect(lineMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatches!.length).toBe(1);

      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:winBrush faceColor="#FF0000"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:pt0 x="4000" y="4000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:pt1 x="16000" y="16000"/>');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. P1: Image FILL Crop Verification
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: Image FILL Crop Verification", () => {
    const widePngBytes = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d,
      0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // width = 200
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // height = 100
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    const tallPngBytes = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d,
      0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // width = 100
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // height = 200
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    it("should calculate correct imgClip bounds for horizontal overflow in HWPX (left & right crop)", async () => {
      const imageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Crop Test Wide",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img1",
                name: "Wide Image in Square Box",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "sample_wide.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "sample_wide.png": {
            id: "sample_wide.png",
            name: "sample_wide.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: widePngBytes,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imageDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const imgClipMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:imgClip\s+([^>]+)\/>/);
      expect(imgClipMatch).toBeDefined();
      const clipAttrs = imgClipMatch![1];

      const leftVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/left="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      const rightVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/right="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      expect(leftVal).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(rightVal).toBeLessThan(20000);
    });

    it("should calculate correct imgClip bounds for vertical overflow in HWPX (top & bottom crop)", async () => {
      const imageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Crop Test Tall",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img2",
                name: "Tall Image in Square Box",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "sample_tall.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "sample_tall.png": {
            id: "sample_tall.png",
            name: "sample_tall.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: tallPngBytes,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imageDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const imgClipMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:imgClip\s+([^>]+)\/>/);
      expect(imgClipMatch).toBeDefined();
      const clipAttrs = imgClipMatch![1];

      const topVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/top="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      const bottomVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/bottom="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      expect(topVal).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(bottomVal).toBeLessThan(20000);
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 4. P1: Text Vertical Alignment F-12
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: Text Vertical Alignment F-12", () => {
    it("should preserve TOP, CENTER, BOTTOM alignment across Canvas IR, DOCX, and HWPX", async () => {
      const figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
      const textTop = {
        id: "text:top",
        name: "Top Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Top Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "TOP" },
      };
      const textCenter = {
        id: "text:center",
        name: "Center Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Center Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 150, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "CENTER" },
      };
      const textBottom = {
        id: "text:bottom",
        name: "Bottom Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Bottom Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 250, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "BOTTOM" },
      };

      const doc = figmaParser.parse({
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Vertical Align Comparison",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [textTop, textCenter, textBottom],
      } as any);

      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[0].verticalAlign).toBe("top");
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[1].verticalAlign).toBe("center");
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[2].verticalAlign).toBe("bottom");

      // Verify DOCX VML emits exact v-text-anchor tags
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:top");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:middle");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:bottom");

      // Verify HWPX emits exact vertAlign attributes
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="TOP"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="CENTER"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="BOTTOM"');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 5. P2: HWPX Rounded Corners F-13
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P2: HWPX Rounded Corners F-13", () => {
    it("should compute accurate ratio for cornerRadius=0, 8, 24 and cards with stroke & fill", async () => {
      const cardsDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Corner Radius Scale Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "c0",
                name: "Sharp Rect (r=0)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#FFFFFF", cornerRadius: 0 },
              },
              {
                id: "c8",
                name: "Round Card (r=8)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 160, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 2,
                style: { fillColor: "#F0F0F0", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 8 },
              },
              {
                id: "c24",
                name: "Round Card (r=24)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 270, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 3,
                style: { fillColor: "#E0E0E0", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 24 },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(cardsDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const rectMatches = Array.from(secXml.matchAll(/<hp:rect\b[^>]*\bratio="(\d+)"/g));
      expect(rectMatches.length).toBe(3);

      const ratio0 = parseInt(rectMatches[0][1], 10);
      const ratio8 = parseInt(rectMatches[1][1], 10);
      const ratio24 = parseInt(rectMatches[2][1], 10);

      expect(ratio0).toBe(0);
      expect(ratio8).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(ratio24).toBeGreaterThan(ratio8);
    });
  });
});

================================================================================
FILE: tests/phase17-quality-integration.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument, CanvasElement } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 17 Quality & Integration Test Suite", () => {
  // =========================================================================
  // 1. Native HWPX Line Rendering for LINE Elements
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Track A: HWPX Native Line Rendering", () => {
    it("should render Figma LINE nodes as native <hp:line> with pt0 and pt1 in HWPX", async () => {
      const lineDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Native Line Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "div_1",
                name: "Horizontal Divider",
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 100, width: 400, height: 1 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { strokeColor: "#336699", strokeWidth: 2 },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(lineDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Verify <hp:line> tag presence and attributes
      const lineMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b[\s\S]*?<\/hp:line>/);
      expect(lineMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('color="#336699"');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('style="SOLID"');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('<hc:pt0 x="0" y="0"/>');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('<hc:pt1 x="40000" y="100"/>');
      expect(lineMatch![0]).toContain('isReverseHV="0"');
      // Ensure it did not fall back to <hp:rect>
      expect(secXml).not.toContain('<hp:rect');
    });

    it("should strictly cap rounded rectangle ratio to 50 for pills and semi-circles", async () => {
      const pillDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Pill Shape Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "pill_1",
                name: "Badge Pill",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 120, height: 40 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#007ACC", cornerRadius: 50 }, // Corner radius exceeds minDim / 2 (20)
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(pillDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const rectMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:rect\b[^>]*\bratio="(\d+)"/);
      expect(rectMatch).toBeDefined();
      const ratio = parseInt(rectMatch![1], 10);
      expect(ratio).toBe(50); // Capped at exactly 50 per KS X 6101 schema
    });

    it("should emit 75 HWPU/px image dimensions and both hp:imgDim and hp:inImgDim", async () => {
      // 100x50 PNG sample bytes
      const pngHeader = Buffer.from([
        0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a, // PNG magic
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d, // IHDR length (13)
        0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52, // IHDR
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // width = 100 px
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x32, // height = 50 px
        0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, // 8-bit truecolor
        0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, // CRC placeholder
      ]);

      const imgDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Image Dimension Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img_dim_test",
                name: "Test Image",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 50 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "res_test_img.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "res_test_img.png": {
            id: "res_test_img.png",
            name: "res_test_img.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: pngHeader,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imgDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // 100 px * 75 = 7500 HWPU; 50 px * 75 = 3750 HWPU
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:imgDim dimwidth="7500" dimheight="3750"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('binaryItemIDRef="BIN0001"');
      // Phase 20 OWPML Schema Fix: ensure spurious inImgDim and binRef are not emitted
      expect(secXml).not.toContain('<hp:inImgDim');
      expect(secXml).not.toContain('binRef="BIN0001"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:imgRect>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:pt1 x="10000" y="0"/>');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. Track B: Mixed Orientation & Multi-Page Sections
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Track B: Mixed Orientation Multi-Page Documents", () => {
    it("should generate proper section break in DOCX when page orientations differ", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Orientation Document",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
            elements: [
              {
                id: "p1_text",
                name: "Page 1 Portrait",
                type: "text",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 50 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                textRuns: [{ text: "Portrait Page 1", fontFamily: "Calibri", fontSizePt: 12 }],
              },
            ],
          },
          {
            pageNumber: 2,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 841.89, height: 595.28 },
            backgroundColor: "#F9F9F9",
            elements: [
              {
                id: "p2_text",
                name: "Page 2 Landscape",
                type: "text",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 300, height: 50 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                textRuns: [{ text: "Landscape Page 2", fontFamily: "Calibri", fontSizePt: 12 }],
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      // Verify Page 1 paragraph has inline sectPr with w:type="nextPage" and portrait size
      expect(docXml).toContain('<w:type w:val="nextPage"/>');
      expect(docXml).toContain('w:w="11906" w:h="16838" w:orient="portrait"');

      // Verify final document sectPr has landscape size
      expect(docXml).toContain('w:w="16838" w:h="11906" w:orient="landscape"');
    });

    it("should generate per-section hp:secPr in HWPX when page orientations differ", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Orientation Document HWPX",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
            elements: [],
          },
          {
            pageNumber: 2,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 841.89, height: 595.28 },
            backgroundColor: "#F0F0F0",
            elements: [],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const secPrMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:secPr\b/g);
      expect(secPrMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(secPrMatches!.length).toBe(2);

      // Page 1 is NARROWLY, Page 2 is WIDELY
      expect(secXml).toContain('landscape="NARROWLY" width="59528" height="84189"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('landscape="WIDELY" width="84189" height="59528"');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. Real-World Fixture Conversion
  // =========================================================================
  describe("Track B: Real-World Fixture Integrity", () => {
    it("should convert real-world proposal doc fixture cleanly into DOCX and HWPX", async () => {
      const fixturePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/real-world/sample-a-proposal-doc.json");
      expect(fs.existsSync(fixturePath)).toBe(true);

      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(fixturePath, "utf-8"));
      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Real World Proposal" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      expect(doc.pages.length).toBe(1);
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
      const textElements = doc.pages[0].elements.filter((e) => e.type === "text");
      expect(textElements.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);

      // Render DOCX
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      expect(docxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docxXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(docxXml).toContain("2026 차세대 엔터프라이즈 AI 자동화 구축 제안서");
      expect(docxXml).toContain("1. 핵심 기대 효과 및 도입 타당성");

      // Render HWPX
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      expect(hwpxBuf.length).toBeGreaterThan(1000);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const hwpxSecXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(hwpxSecXml).toContain("2026 차세대 엔터프라이즈 AI 자동화 구축 제안서");
      expect(hwpxSecXml).toContain("1. 핵심 기대 효과 및 도입 타당성");
    });
  });
});

FILE: tests/phase19-docx-text-fidelity.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import {
  DocxCanvasRenderer,
  FigmaCanvasParser,
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasElement,
} from "../src/canvas/index.js";

describe("Phase 19: DOCX Text Fidelity Finalization Suite", () => {
  const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();

  it("Item 1: Fixed-width Korean text automatically wraps and sets mso-wrap-style:square", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Fixed Width Korean Wrap",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {},
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
          elements: [
            {
              id: "txt_wrap_korean",
              name: "Korean Paragraph",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 40, y: 40, width: 200, height: 60 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              textRuns: [
                {
                  text: "주의: 본 균열 폭 측정 결과는 GSD 기준의 AI 영상 분석 추정치이며 물리적 정밀 측정을 요합니다.",
                  fontFamily: "맑은 고딕",
                  fontSizePt: 10,
                  fontWeight: 400,
                  color: "#1F2937",
                  lineHeightPt: 14,
                },
              ],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const buf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Must have mso-wrap-style:square to enforce WordWrap in Word COM
    expect(xml).toContain("mso-wrap-style:square");
    expect(xml).not.toContain('id="s_0_0"[^>]*mso-wrap-style:none');
    expect(xml).toContain("주의: 본 균열 폭 측정 결과는");
  });

  it("Item 2: Explicit line breaks are strictly preserved as <w:br/>", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Explicit Breaks",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, orientation: "portrait", unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {},
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          backgroundColor: "#FFFFFF",
          elements: [
            {
              id: "txt_breaks",
              name: "Explicit Breaks Text",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 40, y: 40, width: 300, height: 80 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              textRuns: [
                {
                  text: "첫 번째 줄\n두 번째 줄\n세 번째 줄",
                  fontFamily: "Pretendard",
                  fontSizePt: 12,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  color: "#111827",
                  lineHeightPt: 18,
                },
              ],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };

    const buf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    expect(xml).toContain("첫 번째 줄</w:t><w:br/><w:t xml:space=\"preserve\">두 번째 줄</w:t><w:br/><w:t xml:space=\"preserve\">세 번째 줄");
  });

  it("Item 3: Auto-width text (WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT) preserves single-line rendering with mso-wrap-style:none to prevent unwanted wrapping and clipping", async () => {
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ scalePolicy: "fit-a4" });
    const figmaJson = {

    const xml = await zip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

    // Single-line auto-width text must NOT auto-wrap to prevent Word font variance clipping
    expect(xml).toContain("mso-wrap-style:none");
    expect(xml).toContain("mso-fit-shape-to-text:t");
  });

  it("Item 10: Non-text elements (backgrounds, lines, images) preserve mso-wrap-style:none", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {

      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      resources: {
        "dot-img": {
          id: "dot-img",
          name: "dot.png",
          mimeType: "image/png",
          data: Buffer.from("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mNk+M9QDwADhgGAWjR9awAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==", "base64"),
        },
